| o | defined | symbol | cache | **ColdCache (rehydrate/compact cold prompt-cache reason)** - ColdCache is the rehydrate reason and compact option indicating that the prompt/message-span cache is cold, so a prompt-cache preserving path no longer has warm-cache value to protect. |
| o | defined | symbol | cache | **coldMessageSpanCache (gateway idle-gap witness)** - coldMessageSpanCache is the gateway metrics helper that witnesses whether a trace's message-span cache has aged past the provider TTL. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **context.Context plumbing helpers (stdlib)** - exec.CommandContext is the Go standard-library constructor that threads a cancellable context.Context into a spawned subprocess, one of the stdlib context-plumbing helpers fak uses to carry deadlines and cancellation through calls. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **BaseContextPlan (syspromptmmu)** - syspromptmmu.BaseContextPlan returns the embedded-PromptSegment projection of the base system context (TierSpine then the TierPolicy floor) as a byte-identical, no-wire-mutation plan. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **AutoSizeContextPlan (compute sizer)** - compute.AutoSizeContextPlan derives the largest context-token count that fits available memory and returns the per-context KV/scratch MemoryPlan for it, reading geometry from a compute.ContextSizeConfig (KV/scratch configs plus the model's declared MaxContext). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **putCtx (modelengine pipeline)** - putCtx is the context.Context carried through the modelengine PipelineEngine.run / lifecycle adapter for one put (decode) request, scoping cancellation of that pipeline step. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ctxParts (Slack post builder)** - ctxParts is the local string slice that the Slack-post render functions (blockerpost, cachevaluepost, milestonepost, marketing) accumulate into a single Slack context block's text (ref, posted-by, etc.). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **AppendContext (slackmeta block)** - slackmeta.AppendContext appends a Slack context block carrying the signal-to-noise line to a message's blocks, unless an existing block already includes one. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **dos_recall (MCP recall-check tool)** - dos_recall is the DOS MCP tool (named across loopmap/rehydrate/accounts comments as the kernel-side equivalent) that re-verifies a recalled memory against the tree at read time before it is trusted, since a memory reflects only what was true when written. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **maxCtx (ctxdemo peak length)** - maxCtx is the local running maximum the ctxdemo / longctxbench / sessionbench tools track as they scan scenarios for the largest context length any scenario reaches. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **tombstoned_by_context_control (page-in refusal reason)** - tombstoned_by_context_control is the ctxplan/contextq/memq refusal reason string returned when a span is denied page-in because it was suppressed by a context-control tombstone (recall.ErrTombstoned). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **BUDGET_CONTEXT_EXHAUSTED (session reset reason)** - BUDGET_CONTEXT_EXHAUSTED is the session/gateway reset reason constant (session.ReasonBudgetContext, gateway.sessionReasonBudgetContext) emitted when ContextTokensLeft hits zero, returning a managed reset directive. |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **CompactionBudget (history-rewrite threshold)** - CompactionBudget is the gateway exit-summary field (Config.CompactHistoryBudget) holding the resident-token threshold the history rewrite fires past (0 means the compaction lever is OFF and the body is forwarded byte-for-byte). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextBudget (TUI session field)** - ContextBudget is the cmd/fak TUI report field (json context_budget) carrying a session's configured context-token budget for display in the overview/sessions panes. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CtxWith / CtxWithout (tokendemo counters)** - CtxWith and CtxWithout are the cmd/tokendemo per-scenario counters of the model tokens added to context with fak's gate engaged (a refused/cached path) versus without it (the tool re-runs and its full result lands), used to show fak only saves on a refused bad call or a re-read. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **SkillContextRecord (contextq descriptor)** - contextq.SkillContextRecord is the procedural-memory descriptor for one skill invocation, naming the producer and view-cache producer for that skill's context entry. |
| o | defined | doc-term | context-ctx | **ContextBench (academic context-retrieval benchmark)** - ContextBench is an external academic code-agent context-retrieval benchmark (separate repo, Nanjing University + UCL research) named in docs/benchmarks/LEGACY-BENCH-INDEX.md as a context-retrieval comparison surface. |
| o | defined | doc-term | context-ctx | **ContextCite (attribution research method)** - ContextCite is an external 2024 research method (Cohen-Wang et al., "ContextCite: Attributing Model Generation to Context") cited in fak's span-credit research notes as prior art for attributing generation to context spans. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **derived ContextView (cachemeta MemoryView)** - The derived ContextView is the cachemeta materialization view (exercised by TestFromMemoryViewDescribesDerivedContextView) that FromContextPage / FromMemoryView describes for a ContextPage span when deciding cache serveability. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxtape (portable context-tape renderer)** - ctxtape is the portable context-tape renderer (shipped in v0.33.0) that renders any trajectory, RSI, or education run as a portable context tape. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **contextevents (context-ctx identifier)** - contextevents is the concrete context-ctx identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring context-ctx concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **fakcompactionshedtokens (context-ctx identifier)** - fakcompactionshedtokens is the concrete context-ctx identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring context-ctx concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **prioritycontext (context-ctx identifier)** - prioritycontext is the concrete context-ctx identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring context-ctx concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextTokensLeft (session budget)** - ContextTokensLeft is the session.Budget field holding the remaining prompt/context-token budget a served session has before context-budget exhaustion triggers a reset directive. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextTokens (session usage)** - ContextTokens is the per-turn usage field on gateway.SessionUsage / session.Usage carrying the prompt/context-window token count to debit from the session budget. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **RequestContextChange (recall)** - RequestContextChange is the recall.Session method that applies a safe, negative-only context mutation — recording a tombstone that suppresses one page from future model-visible recall without deleting bytes. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextActionTombstone** - ContextActionTombstone is the recall.ContextAction constant ("tombstone_page") naming the one shipped, deliberately negative-only context mutation that narrows what may enter future context. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxplanbench (planner bench cmd)** - ctxplanbench is the cmd/ctxplanbench benchmark that measures the ctxplan planned VIEW (compaction count, recovered tokens) over real Claude Code session transcripts. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextChanges (recall ledger)** - ContextChanges is the recall.Session accessor (and Manifest field) returning the applied, durable context-control ledger — the list of tombstone rows recorded against the core image. |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **ContextPollutionRate (bench KPI)** - ContextPollutionRate is the bench KPI computed as the rate of ctxmmu quarantines per tool call in the gates-on run. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxdemo (multi-agent demo cmd)** - ctxdemo is the cmd/ctxdemo live on-box demonstrator that runs a catalog of multi-agent shapes to show fak's prefill/KV-reuse value, reporting per-scenario max context and fak-vs-tuned token work. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **CtxViewBudget (gateway knob)** - CtxViewBudget is the gateway.Config field that, when > 0, wires the ctxplan context planner into the live serve/guard loop and materializes each turn's history as an O(1) planned view under this resident-token budget. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ProveRecallInput (vcachechain)** - ProveRecallInput is the vcachechain struct holding the caller-supplied calibration (prefix tokens, unit tokens, read-multiplier, siblings) that the recall economics proof is run over. |
| o | defined | cli-verb | context-ctx | **cmdRecall (fak recall verb)** - cmdRecall is the cmd/fak handler for `fak recall` — the offline session-recall demo that writes a session through the gate, reloads it in a fresh Session, and demonstrates the rung-0/rung-4 poison-refusal guarantees. |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **CompactionFired (gateway metric)** - CompactionFired is the gateway metrics counter of compaction attempts that fired — the turn body was rewritten with the protected prefix shipped byte-identical. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **contextBudgetAxis (TUI formatter)** - contextBudgetAxis is the cmd/fak formatter that renders a context-token budget value as a short axis cell ("inf" for <0, "off" for 0, else the number) for ps/overview/session rows. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **context-budget-tokens (guard flag)** - contextBudgetTokens is the `fak guard --context-budget-tokens` flag that seeds the guard session with a prompt/context-token budget so exhaustion returns a reset directive with a continuation id. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextTokensCap (budget denominator)** - ContextTokensCap is the session.Budget field recording the configured context-budget size — the denominator the pre-exhaustion warning measures consumed-share against (0 means no context budget). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextWindowTokens (usage method)** - ContextWindowTokens is the agent.Usage method that computes the prompt/context size to count against a long-session budget, adding Anthropic cache_read/cache_creation tokens back onto input_tokens. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxbench (security-gate corpus cmd)** - ctxbench is the cmd/ctxbench tool that runs fak's security gates over a corpus of tool calls and reports the injection/quarantine outcomes. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **handleFakContextChange (gateway route)** - handleFakContextChange is the gateway HTTP handler for POST /v1/fak/context/change that records a requester-initiated tombstone against a persisted recall core image. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **freshContextTokens (guard reseed)** - freshContextTokens is the cmd/fak guard budget-restarter field holding the fresh context-token budget to reseed onto a continuation trace after a budget-triggered managed reset. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **InjectionInContext (agent harness)** - InjectionInContext is the agent loop-result boolean recording that a tool result carrying a prompt injection actually reached the model in the context. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **longctxbench (long-context floor cmd)** - longctxbench is the cmd/longctxbench tool that renders the exact, contention-free token-work floor for ultra-long-context / agent-city regimes across a prefix/turns/agents ladder. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **MaxContext (swebench geometry)** - MaxContext is the swebench.Geometry method giving the largest context length a workload geometry reaches, computed as Prefix + Turns*(Decode+Result). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **NewRecallBackend (memq)** - NewRecallBackend is the memq constructor that builds the negative-only recall backend — a tombstone store that drives suppression through recall.RequestContextChange persisted to a directory. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **PlanRecall (vcachechain)** - PlanRecall is the vcachechain M4 entry point that, given a prefix DAG, a recall request, and an enable flag, returns the recall verdict plus the economics and cold-tail replay schedule. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **RopeOrigContext (model config)** - RopeOrigContext is the model.Config field (rope_scaling_original_max_position_embeddings) holding the original pre-scaling max position / context length used as the reference when applying RoPE scaling. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxresidency (residency query package)** - ctxresidency is the internal package providing the context-residency query: a pure, witnessable READ that composes kvmmu, ctxmmu, and cachemeta to classify each span resident/evictable/held with its evict blast radius. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxplandemo (planner demo cmd)** - ctxplandemo is the cmd/ctxplandemo runnable demonstrator for the context PLANNER, treating a transcript as a plannable store and showing the planned view it materializes. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **observeCtxViewRewrite (ctx-view metric witness)** - gateway.observeCtxViewRewrite is the metrics helper that records a successful ctxplan planned-view rewrite as ctx-view events, dropped turns, and shed resident-token estimates without incrementing compact-history counters. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxknobs (manual-overlay counter)** - ctxknobs is the internal/ctxknobs R1 manual-overlay counter for the zero-knob automatic-context epic (#2199): it walks cmd/fak flag/env registrations and .claude/skills/*/SKILL.md, classifies each context/cache/session-budget overlay as operator-debug or user-required, and ratchets the user-required count down against a frozen baseline. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **isContextFlagName (ctxknobs classifier)** - isContextFlagName is the internal/ctxknobs name-only classifier deciding whether a flag/env base name is a context/cache/session BUDGET knob - matching 'ctx', 'context', 'compact', 'managed-cache', or a budget-qualified 'view'/'token'/'session'/'cache' name. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxplans (context-plan-required lint)** - ctxplans is the internal/ctxplans R4 advisory lint (#2202, epic #2198), run under `fak index ctxplans` / `make ci`, that scans the tree for the CONTEXT-PLAN-REQUIRED marker vocabulary. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **DefaultCtxViewBudget** - DefaultCtxViewBudget is the internal/agent constant (8000) that CtxViewPlanner/CtxViewSession fall back to as the O(1) resident-token window whenever no explicit budget is supplied. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **fak_context_restore (MCP tool)** - fak_context_restore is the internal/gateway MCP tool id wired to restoreContext: given a content-address handle a compaction tombstone embedded, it pages the dropped originating-task bytes back in, honoring the trust gate. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **fak_context_spans (MCP tool)** - fak_context_spans is the internal/gateway MCP tool id wired to contextSpans: it enumerates the content-addressed dropped spans a session trace currently holds as SAFE, byte-free rows, without paging any bytes back in. |
| o | defined | concept | context-ctx | **context_compression (ablate/cachevalue plane)** - context_compression is the Plane tag (internal/ablate's feature catalog, internal/cachevaluereport, cmd/fak's cachevalue-status) naming the SHRINK-BEFORE-CONTEXT plane: internal/headroom's pluggable Compressor reduces tool-result bytes before they ever reach a model turn. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **contextjoin (vcache lifecycle JOIN)** - contextjoin is internal/vcacheobserve's JOIN engine (`fak vcache context-join`, JoinSchema "fak.vcache.contextjoin.v1", issue #1607) correlating provider prompt-cache cost changes against the managed-context LIFECYCLE (resets, compactions, page faults, prefix mutations) to attribute each change to planning or provider behavior. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextRestoreRequest** - ContextRestoreRequest is the internal/gateway fak_context_restore MCP argument shape: the content-address handle a compaction tombstone embedded, and an optional trace id (omitted resolves to the gateway default trace). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **restoreContext (gateway method)** - restoreContext is the internal/gateway Server method resolving a fak_context_restore call: it looks up a content-addressed stashed span under Server.ctxRestoreMu and pages the bytes back in, honoring the trust gate (ErrRestoreRefused for sealed/tombstoned, ErrRestoreMiss for unknown). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **Server.ctxRestore (stash table)** - ctxRestore is the internal/gateway Server field (map[string]*sessionCtxRestore) holding, per session trace, the ordered stash of content-addressed originating-task bytes a fired compaction tombstone dropped. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **Server.ctxRestoreMu** - ctxRestoreMu is the internal/gateway Server mutex guarding the ctxRestore stash table and the per-trace sessionCtxRestore entries it holds. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **contextSpans (gateway method)** - contextSpans is the internal/gateway Server method resolving a fak_context_spans call: it reads Server.ctxRestore under ctxRestoreMu and maps each stashed restoreEntry to a byte-free CtxSpan row (id, descriptor, size, evidence edges, sealed/tombstoned/restorable), in stash order. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **contextstatus (scorecardpane status line)** - contextstatus.go (internal/scorecardpane, issue #1577) renders ContextStatusSignals - six live managed-context counters (resident tokens, budget tokens, cache state, reset count, query-needed count, stale-assumption count) plus an already-classified severity - into ONE deterministic status line for `fak info` / the split-pane overlay. |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **ContextSwitchesPerSec (stallscan OS metric)** - ContextSwitchesPerSec is the internal/stallscan struct field (and `fak stallscan` report line) reporting the OS SCHEDULER's measured process context-switches per second, gathered from Windows performance counters. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **totalContext (sessionaudit aggregate)** - totalContext is the internal/sessionaudit local aggregate (Input + CacheRead + CacheCreate) computed once per report to populate TotalContextTokens and the cache-read-share ratio. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CompactionBoundary (trajctl marker)** - CompactionBoundary is the internal/trajctl function returning one boundary-marker TurnSample row per open objective (method "compaction-boundary", version 1), so the trajectory curve keeps continuity across a context-reset. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **contextBudgetLimit (guard resolved budget)** - contextBudgetLimit is the cmd/fak guard resolved local holding the effective context-token budget for a run, seeded from --context-budget-tokens or a resume budget envelope's ContextTokensLeft, and threaded through reset/restart wiring. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **context-snapshot (vcache flag/field)** - context-snapshot is the `fak vcache` --context-snapshot flag (and cachevalue-status ContextSnapshot JSON field) pointing at a separate context-plane witness snapshot path, defaulting to a well-known path so a no-key replay can prove context without overwriting provider telemetry. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxhealth (trajhook verdict scorer)** - ctxhealth.go (internal/trajhook, issue #3098) is the context-health VERDICT scorer folding a corpus row's deterministic behavioral fields (verdicts, args digests, queries, turn counts) into exactly one HEALTHY/STALL/DRIFT/DETOUR_OVERRUN Finding per trace, reusing trajctl's existing closed vocabulary. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CtxSpansResult (fak_context_spans reply)** - ctxspans.go / CtxSpansResult is the internal/gateway shape for the fak_context_spans reply: the ordered list of CtxSpan rows (byte-free, sha256-hex id, descriptor, size, evidence edges, restorable bit) a trace currently holds, oldest first. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CtxStore (recall's ctxplan.Store adapter)** - CtxStore is the internal/recall type (ctxplan_store.go) adapting a reloaded *Session into a ctxplan.Store - the first REAL store implementation the ctxplan planner views, so a sealed page reads as a Sealed Span and a tombstoned page as a Tombstoned one. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ctxValueForLocked** - ctxValueForLocked is the internal/gateway Server method that lazily mints (or returns) the per-trace sessionCtxValue record, bounding the table exactly like the sibling per-session tables, and must be called with ctxValueMu already held. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **Server.ctxValueMu** - ctxValueMu is the internal/gateway Server mutex guarding the per-trace managed-context value table (sessionCtxValue records) that ctxValueForLocked/observeCtxValue read and write. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CtxValueReport** - CtxValueReport is the internal/gateway struct for one session's multi-level managed-context report (tokens/turns/session/step_advice/wakeup/footprint), returned by GET /v1/fak/ctxvalue and the fak_context_value MCP tool. |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **ctxViewDropped** - ctxViewDropped is the internal/gateway metrics counter (guarded by ctxViewMu) accumulating tokens the ctxplan planned-view rewrite path dropped, folded via observeCtxViewRewrite and surfaced in the metrics snapshot as "dropped". |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **ctxViewEvents** - ctxViewEvents is the internal/gateway metrics counter (guarded by ctxViewMu) counting how many ctxplan planned-view rewrites fired, incremented once per rewrite by observeCtxViewRewrite and surfaced in the metrics snapshot as "events". |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **Server.ctxViewMu** - ctxViewMu is the internal/gateway Server mutex guarding the ctxplan planned-view rewrite counters (ctxViewEvents/ctxViewDropped/ctxViewShed) - a CONTEXT-plane witness distinct from the history-compaction accumulators it sits beside in metrics.go. |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **ctxViewShed** - ctxViewShed is the internal/gateway metrics counter (guarded by ctxViewMu) accumulating the estimated ShedTokens a ctxplan planned-view rewrite removed, folded via observeCtxViewRewrite and surfaced in the metrics snapshot as "shed". |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **FromContextPage** - FromContextPage is the internal/cachemeta adapter function turning a durable recall/context ContextPage into a cachemeta Entry, mapping a quarantined page to AdmissionQuarantine and tagging it with session/step/descriptor labels. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **HardContextCap (ctxplan envelope field)** - HardContextCap is the internal/ctxplan EffectiveContextEnvelope field naming the provider's advertised limit on request+output - a hard CAP, never the target resident budget the default path actually aims for. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **noteCtxValueTTL1h** - noteCtxValueTTL1h is the internal/gateway Server method recording that the 1h prompt-cache TTL-upgrade rung fired for a trace, so a later cache-creation-token counter read BEFORE this turn's write can fold correctly. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **observeCtxValue** - observeCtxValue is the internal/gateway Server method that rolls one served turn's usage (uncached prompt, cache read, cache creation, completion, compacted flag) into the session's managed-context CtxValueReport record. |
| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **recall_value_witnessed (memvaluescore KPI)** - recall_value_witnessed is the internal/memvaluescore KPI key (Group "value") measuring whether a memory-store recall actually got used, witnessed rather than merely claimed. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ResidentContextTokens** - ResidentContextTokens is the JSON field (on the gateway's outbound turn envelope and on session.Usage/session.Observation) carrying one turn's resident prompt/context token count - the debit applied against a session's context budget. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **maxCtxValueSessions** - maxCtxValueSessions is the internal/gateway constant (8192) bounding the per-session ctxValue table; on overflow the whole table takes a generational reset, mirroring maxCtxRestoreSessions for the restore stash. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **compaction_ab (ablate A/B arm)** - compaction_ab.go (internal/ablate, #2805) is the LIVE compaction ON-vs-OFF A/B arm: a controlled split over MATCHED real guard sessions that reports the net dollar delta (shed saving MINUS the cache-creation burst compaction causes when it rewrites history), never gross shed. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **compaction_on_vs_off (AB arm id)** - compaction_on_vs_off is the internal/ablate CompactionABArmID string constant naming the sweep-row arm for the ON-vs-OFF net-of-burst dollar delta the compaction_ab file reports. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CompactionReport (usage-ledger fold)** - CompactionReport is the internal/gatewayusageledger struct folding compaction-ledger rows into a (budget x length-band) segmentation over a --since window, carrying exit/quarantined row counts and per-segment shed totals for `fak cachevalue compaction`. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **FoldCompaction (usage-ledger)** - FoldCompaction is the internal/gatewayusageledger function that folds ledger rows into the (budget x length-band) CompactionReport segmentation over the whole --since window (FoldCompactionByPeriod with no time bucketing). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CtxExpense (expense verdict)** - CtxExpense is the internal/gateway struct surfacing the per-session expense verdict beside the OBSERVED tokens in a CtxValueReport: a closed 3-level decision (ok/warn/block) graded on the ESTIMATED per-turn as-sent volume, never the observed resident count. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **ctxExpenseWarn (Server threshold)** - ctxExpenseWarn is the internal/gateway Server field holding the effective per-turn-as-sent-volume token threshold above which assessCtxExpense returns the warn tier (0 = the built-in 120k default; negative = that tier disabled). |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **ctxExpenseBlock (Server threshold)** - ctxExpenseBlock is the internal/gateway Server field holding the effective per-turn-as-sent-volume token threshold above which assessCtxExpense returns the block tier (0 = the built-in 180k default; negative = that tier disabled). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ctxExpenseNoteOnce (gate method)** - ctxExpenseNoteOnce is the internal/gateway Server method behind FAK_CTX_EXPENSE_GATE: the opt-in, once-per-session in-band [fak] advisory a block-tier expense verdict emits, telling the agent to checkpoint and end the turn - never refusing the request. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ctxFootprintBytes (raw byte accumulator)** - ctxFootprintBytes is the internal/gateway per-trace accumulator's raw byte record of the latest inbound footprint: the exact, additive byte split (system + built-in tools + MCP tools + history + tail) that CtxValueFootprint renders into ESTIMATED tokens. |
| o | defined | subsystem | context-ctx | **ctxrestore_store (generalized restore router)** - ctxrestore_store.go (internal/gateway, #3062) is the GENERALIZED restore-by-digest router: the adapter that lets a fak_context_restore call resolve bytes from any content-addressed ctxplan.Store, not just the compaction-tombstone stash in ctxrestore.go. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **OpContextRestore (read-op vocab)** - OpContextRestore is the internal/sessionread ReadOp enum value ('context-restore') naming the one full-disclosure read op: pages one dropped span back in by its content-address handle, honoring the ctxplan seal/tombstone trust gate. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **adviseCtxStep (step-advice policy)** - adviseCtxStep is the internal/gateway pure step-advice policy: a closed StepClass verdict (any/bounded/checkpoint/rebuild/unknown) graded on the rolling session's turns, context events, resident growth, and budget - advice-only, never enforced. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CtxValueSnapshot (multi-session body)** - CtxValueSnapshot is the internal/gateway multi-session HTTP body: every tracked session's CtxValueReport rendered at once (optionally filtered to one trace), returned by the fleet-wide ctxvalue endpoint. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **DefaultContextCap (microagent)** - DefaultContextCap is the internal/microagent constant (8192) that NewContext(0) selects as the token ceiling for a mini-SWE-agent's bounded linear history - a usable starting default, not a model-specific window. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **NewContext (microagent constructor)** - NewContext is the internal/microagent constructor building an empty bounded linear-history Context whose FIFO history is capped at tokenCap tokens (DefaultContextCap when 0), enforced with the same tokenizer the guard boot path uses. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **NewCtxStore (recall adapter constructor)** - NewCtxStore is the internal/recall constructor wrapping a reloaded *Session as a ctxplan.Store - the first REAL store implementation the ctxplan planner views, so a sealed page reads as a Sealed Span and a tombstoned page as a Tombstoned one. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **principalFromContext (isolation principal)** - principalFromContext is the internal/gateway function returning the request-scoped isolation principal (or empty) from a Go context.Context - the per-principal boundary that scopes restore/spans reads to the caller's own trace. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **RequiredContext (capacity signal)** - RequiredContext is the internal/modelroute CapacitySignal field carrying the minimum context-window tokens a target model must offer to accept a reroute - the routing-side dual of context-window, checked against a target's usable context. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **SpeculationContext** - Transient state for brainstorm epochs: (Speculative, Epoch, ParentEpoch) marking provisional effects produced under speculation. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ContextChangeRequest** - Negative-only context mutation request: one persisted recall-page tombstone with reason, requestor, and witness. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **RecallProof** - Self-describing proof of a recall cost calculation: shows the gate's work (PrefixTokens, UnitTokens, ReadMult, siblings, cost breakdown). |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ProveRecall** - Pure function that runs the recall cost gate over its inputs and returns a RecallProof showing the gate's work. |
| o | defined | concept | context-ctx | **CtxView** - A planned, budgeted view of context the CtxViewPlanner selects for one turn within a token budget. |
| o | defined | concept | context-ctx | **context window** - The model's hard token budget that the planner allocates resident spans within. |

| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CompactionSolvencyForced (fires forced by the solvency floor)** - internal/gateway/metrics.go: the count of fires the head-anchored burst economics REFUSED and the context-solvency floor forced anyway - a SUBSET of CompactionFired, broken out because an ordinary fire repays in cache dollars while a forced one is knowingly taken at a loss to keep the session inside its context window. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **formatCompactionBudgetNudge (budget line for the debug pane)** - internal/gateway/debug_stats.go: renders the ' budget=spent:... ctx:.../...' nudge line from prompt/completion/cache-read/cache-create against the compaction budget; empty when no budget is set or nothing is resident. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **ScoreCompactionFire (ex-post fire pricing)** - internal/rsiloop/firenettune.go: the PURE fold that prices ONE fire's OBSERVED facts into a scored receipt on the canonical cacheprice basis (#2798) - the same economics CacheBurstBreakEvenTurns prices ex-ante. |

| o | defined | concept | context-ctx | **context health** - context health (internal/scorecardpane/contexthealth.go, issue #1579) is the user-facing severity fold over managed-context signals - one word for how well the managed context program is doing. |

| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **CompactionSnapshotData (gateway context snapshot)** - CompactionSnapshotData is the gateway/debug snapshot payload that records compaction and context-view data for diagnostics and vCache family reporting. |

| o | defined | metric | context-ctx | **TotalContextTokens (sessionaudit aggregate)** - TotalContextTokens is the sessionaudit aggregate field summing context-token usage across the included sessions or turns. |
| o | defined | config | context-ctx | **FAK_CTX_YIELD_CEILING (resident-token yield ceiling)** - internal/compactcohere/compactcohere.go: env ceiling on the resident-token yield terms in the compaction-coherence score; a non-positive value disables every resident-token term. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **injectionReachedContext (injection-in-context flag)** - internal/agentdojo/agentdojo.go injectionReachedContext: whether a prompt-injection payload actually reached the model context (i.e. was NOT quarantined) on an agentdojo trial. |
| o | defined | symbol | context-ctx | **observeCompaction (compaction-outcome metric)** - internal/gateway/metrics_observe.go observeCompaction: records a compaction OUTCOME (a CompactOutcome, and whether compaction was off) into the gateway metrics. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **DecisionJournal (modelroute)** - modelroute.DecisionJournal is the append-only record of model-routing decisions; each entry is a DecisionRecord (rule, strategy, members, scout calls, overhead, digest) built by RecordDecision after a route is chosen, exported to metrics and audit. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **RouteDecision (guardroute)** - guardroute.RouteDecision is the pure verdict over a guard session's RSI fold and worst bucket — whether to route a finding to a queue row or a GitHub issue, at what severity, with a stable cause-key — while cmd/fak guardLocalModelDecision separately decides whether `fak guard` runs a local in-kernel model versus an upstream proxy. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **EditDecision (prompt-mutation schema)** - EditDecision (docs/standards system-prompt-mutation schema) is the reviewable admit|refuse|demote verdict a check yields for a PromptEdit, paired in the schema with OverlayDecision, the reviewable fault|hit|refuse verdict that resolving an OverlayQuery yields. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **growthReapDecision (one growth-gate reap outcome)** - cmd/fak/growthgate.go: one reap outcome record - what a dry run WOULD delete, or what an --apply run reaped or failed to reap; the JSON-tagged accountability record the reap ledger is made of. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **RestartDecision (loopmgr restart verdict)** - internal/loopmgr/restart.go: the structured verdict returned by RestartPolicy.Decide - Restart means a restart is still allowed and is scheduled for RestartAtUnixNano; GiveUp means the caller must stop restarting and surface Reason. Re-deciding inside the backoff window returns the SAME time, so duplicate watchdog ticks debounce instead of spawning a second process. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **TrajectoryWatchdogDecision (agent-stall intervention verdict)** - internal/resume/trajectory_watchdog.go: the clock- and IO-free intervention verdict for a watched agent session. Liveness dominates: a dead session revives from the independently-read fresh anchor; a living healthy or slow session is untouched; a living STALL gets the cheapest reversible nudge first and only escalates after that nudge was tried. |
| o | defined | config | decision | **decision_only (provider action transport mode)** - The default Mode value PlanProviderActionsWithOptions sets on ProviderActionTransport, declaring the provider vcache action plan is advisory-only until a witnessed heartbeat or explicit-cache transport proves byte-identical-prefix and provider deletion capability (the other mode is witnessed_transport). |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **DriveDecision (superloop)** - The single worst-first WorkItem superloop.Drive selects to enter for this invocation: a pure fold over a completed WalkReport carrying Enter, the chosen Member, its Rank (always 1), Action hint, and Satisfied/IssueShortfall so a caller's exit check reads clean only when the walk is truly met. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **NextRotationDecision (accounts rotation)** - The Registry method behind account rotation that returns the full RotationDecision (chosen Seat, OK, and a closed Reason distinguishing an empty pool from a single-bucket pool from an all-others-walled pool, plus the anchor's own room state) so a caller can explain WHY rotation found nothing instead of recomputing the plan. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **TierDecision (issuecost calibration)** - One routing decision the dispatcher made for an issue (Issue, Chosen tier, Required risk floor, Optimal fit) that Calibrate joins against a WitnessedOutcome to classify over-tier versus under-tier dispatch calibration. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **PlacementDecision (cachemeta)** - A cachemeta planner verdict struct (Action, FromTier, ToTier, Directive, EstMoveBytes, Reason) returned by PlanPlacement that recommends whether a KV span should be kept, promoted, demoted, spilled, compress-demoted, or evicted across residency tiers without ever moving bytes itself. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **AppendDecision (witness recorder)** - A witness.Recorder method that appends one JSON-encoded Decision as an append-only git note on refs/notes/fak/decisions, anchored to the concerned commit SHA (or the empty-tree sentinel for a pre-commit refusal), never touching branches or force-pushing. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **GovernorDecision (vcachegov)** - A vcachegov string-typed steady-state verdict (ride_natural, heartbeat_pin, lazy_rebuild, evict, no_cache, explicit_cache) the Governor returns for one prompt prefix, deciding how to keep a provider prompt-cache prefix warm based on arrival rate versus TTL and policy law. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **RedundantDecisionEvent (abi de-double)** - An abi predicate that reports whether an Event is a SECOND emission of a decision another event already carries (a Deny's paired EvDecide, or a require-witness EvDecide on a submitted call), so a decision-stream folder counts each logical decision exactly once. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **ResetDecision (gateway reset)** - The advisory verdict struct (ShouldReset bool, Score 0..1, Reason) returned by gateway ResetPolicy.ResetScore recommending whether a session's KV cache should be RESET to a fresh context instead of CUT by dropping middle turns, logged in shadow mode and acted on by nothing yet. |
| o | defined | subsystem | decision | **decisions ref (witness journal)** - refs/notes/fak/decisions, the dedicated append-only git-notes ref (constant decisionsRef) the witness Recorder writes adjudication Decisions to, one JSON line per note anchored to the concerned commit, forming the durable decision journal. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **DecisionRideNatural / DecisionHeartbeatPin / DecisionLazyRebuild (vCache governor actions)** - The vcachegov governor decision labels that choose the provider-cache action posture for a prefix: ride_natural for enough organic reuse, heartbeat_pin for cheap pinning, and lazy_rebuild for rebuilding only when demand returns. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **PageFaultDecision** - PageFaultDecision (internal/ctxplan/pagefault.go) is DecidePageFault's typed outcome: what to do about a context page-fault event under a PageFaultPolicy. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **SourceDecision** - SourceDecision (internal/resume/source_governor.go) is AdmitSource's verdict on whether a resume source may fire now under its policy. |
| o | defined | symbol | decision | **StaleFactDecision** - StaleFactDecision (internal/recall/stalefact.go) is DetectStaleFact's verdict on whether a recalled fact is stale and which transition to record. |
| o | defined | subsystem | decision | **taskdecision** - Package taskdecision (internal/taskdecision) renders a session's decision log for reset handoffs. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVEvictionAttestation (cachemeta receipt)** - cachemeta.KVEvictionAttestation is the payload-free receipt both eviction tracks produce after a governed K/V eviction — carrying the target entry id, the enforcement scope (KVEvictionScopeExactSpan vs whole-prefix), a degradation bit, and the governance descriptor — minted by the referee's AttestEviction, with EvictBlastRadius the read-only projection of how many positions and dependents one eviction touches. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **PagedKV.Evict (paged exact-span removal)** - PagedKV.Evict (internal/model/paged_evict.go) removes a contiguous logical span from a paged/block KV sequence and re-derives each shifted survivor's K in one rotation from its pre-RoPE Kraw plane so the result is bit-for-bit what a sequence that never saw the span would hold, rebuilding into fresh private blocks (copy-on-write); the agent's InKernelPlanner.EvictKVSpan drives the contiguous twin to quarantine a poisoned per-message span. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVCache.TryEvict (fallible exact-span evict)** - model.KVCache.TryEvict performs a middle-span eviction that surfaces a no-go as a typed RecurrentEvictUnsupportedError (returned for hybrid Gated-DeltaNet caches whose accumulated recurrent state makes mid-span eviction formally unsupported) and leaves the cache unchanged, rather than panicking. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictUnderBudget (budget-gated evictor)** - EvictUnderBudget (kvmmu Context and contextq CapabilityLedger) applies coldest-first eviction ONLY when live residency exceeds the configured budget, freeing positions or capabilities until the held set is back under budget, and is a no-op otherwise. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **StateEvictable (ctxresidency state)** - ctxresidency.StateEvictable is the residency-state constant ("evictable") marking a span whose K/V is in cache but parented by no live cachemeta entry — a clean candidate the evictor may drop without breaking a dependent. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **CAP_EVICT (journal event kind)** - internal/journal CAP_EVICT is the durable journal event-kind constant recording a capability-eviction lifecycle event in the append-only audit trail. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictedCount (deletion-cert field)** - EvictedCount (cmd/deletioncert) is the self-reported number-of-positions-removed field stamped into the signed KV deletion certificate, explicitly fenced as a self-report whose real proof is the max|delta|=0 parity, not the count. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **decisionevict (evict identifier)** - decisionevict is the concrete evict identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring evict concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **evictionStrategy (radix tree victim rule)** - internal/radixkv/eviction_strategy.go: the tree's CURRENT victim rule accessor - it defaults an unset tree (a bare New()) to pure LRU so the zero value keeps its historical behaviour. |
| o | defined | doc-term | evict | **Evictor (informal role term)** - The informal noun kvcost.go's doc comments and the docs corpus use for whichever eviction mechanism is currently making a keep/drop decision (radixkv's LRU pass, the kvmmu coldest-by-EMA policy, or #2239's cost-aware picker) - a role description, not one Go symbol. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **evictToBudget (radixkv Tree LRU budget pass)** - radixkv.(*Tree).evictToBudget is the unexported method that repeatedly picks a victim leaf (LRU by default, or cost-aware under policy EvictionCostAware) and removes it via removeLeaf until the tree's cached token count is back within maxTokens. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVEvictCostAware (KVEvictPolicy replay-policy label)** - compute.KVEvictCostAware is the KVEvictPolicy enum constant selecting #2239's value-aware victim ranking (cheapest-to-lose per KVEvictionCost, LRU tie-break) inside ReplayKVCache and the kvbm benchmark harness. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVEvictionCost (base value-of-keeping score)** - compute.KVEvictionCost(s) is #2239's base per-span value-of-keeping formula (Tokens * (Hits+1) / Bytes, +Inf fail-open on non-positive Bytes) - a higher score means more expensive to lose per byte, so the cheapest-to-lose (lowest-score) span is the eviction victim. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVEvictionCostAged (aging-clock-extended score)** - compute.KVEvictionCostAged(s) in kvcost_aging.go returns s.AgeStamp + KVEvictionCost(s), the GDSF-style aging term that lets recency re-enter the cost ranking (not just break ties) via the pool-stamped AgingClock value frozen on the span's last reference. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVEvictionCostPinned (pin/hint-extended score)** - compute.KVEvictionCostPinned(s) in kvcost_pin.go extends KVEvictionCost with #2673's TTL-decaying PinBoost term and the #2225 Hint (precious/ephemeral) bounded multiplier, folding pin economics into the cost-of-losing additively rather than a hard bypass, and reduces exactly to KVEvictionCost when PinBoost==0 and Hint==HintNone. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVEvictLRU (KVEvictPolicy replay-policy label)** - compute.KVEvictLRU is the KVEvictPolicy enum zero-value selecting pure recency-only eviction inside ReplayKVCache/kvbm - the baseline #2239's cost-aware mode must beat on a trace with hot/cold structure. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVSpanEvictor (agent quarantine-bridge interface)** - agent.KVSpanEvictor is the Go interface (EvictKVSpan) InKernelPlanner implements to bridge a tool-result QUARANTINE (#579) into the kvmmu governance layer: it rebuilds the transcript as labeled per-message K/V segments on a fresh model.Session and evicts the poisoned segment via the proven model.KVCache.Evict, engaged only when FAK_INKERNEL_KVMMU opts in. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **PickEvictionVictim (base cost-aware victim selector)** - compute.PickEvictionVictim(spans) returns the index of the cheapest-to-lose evictable span per KVEvictionCost (skipping Pinned/Leased spans, LRU tie-break, -1 when nothing is evictable) - the function #2239 names as the eventual replacement for radixkv's and modelengine's pure-LRU victim choice behind FAK_NATIVE_KV_* flags. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictPrefix (radixkv token-path eviction)** - EvictPrefix (internal/radixkv/radixkv.go) is verdict-driven eviction by TOKEN PATH: it walks the longest cached prefix of the given token sequence and evicts the deepest cached node on that path (its whole subtree), returning tokens freed. It is the EvictNode seam for a caller that holds the poisoned token sequence rather than a *node handle - exactly the in-kernel planner, which caches by token path and (because radixkv's node type is unexported) keeps no node references to feed EvictNode directly. EvictPrefixNS scopes the same walk to a namespace (#3889). |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictionCostAware (radixkv production policy)** - radixkv.EvictionCostAware (internal/radixkv/radixkv.go) is the EvictionPolicy enum constant that selects cost-aware victim selection in the PRODUCTION radix prefix-cache tree: victimLeaf dispatches to costAwareLeaf (cheapest-to-lose per compute.KVEvictionCost, LRU tie-break) instead of pure-LRU lruLeaf. Opt-in via NewWithEvictionPolicy while the #2666 production wiring lands. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **evictLocked (blob/content-store eviction)** - evictLocked (internal/blob/store.go and internal/blobfs/store.go) drops unpinned content-addressed digests/blobs oldest-first (LRU) until the resident footprint is back within maxBytes, called under the owner's lock (the Locked suffix is the Go concurrency convention). Pinned digests are never in the LRU order, so they are never evicted; if only pinned digests remain, the loop stops (it bounds the leak, not the live working set). |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictColdest (kvmmu)** - EvictColdest (internal/kvmmu/attention.go) drops the coldest-by-attention-EMA UNPINNED KV spans from a Context until at least targetPositions cache positions are freed (or no unpinned live spans remain), excluding pinned (e.g. system) spans from the candidate set entirely. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **ActionEvict (cachemeta)** - ActionEvict (internal/cachemeta/placement.go) is the PlacementAction constant "evict" — the placement planner's verdict to drop a cache entry entirely and recompute it on demand (FromTier -> TierRecompute), the last resort taken after demote/spill/compress_demote are exhausted or refused. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **evictExcessLocked (bounded-FIFO)** - evictExcessLocked (internal/ctxmmu/mmu.go, and the parallel guards in preflight/normgate/secretgate) drops the OLDEST held entries FIFO until the held set is back within its maxHeld bound, releasing each dropped entry's resource (CAS pin / handle) and bumping an evicted counter — a memory bound on a quarantine / negative-cache, called under the owner's lock. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictKV (speculative rollback)** - EvictKV (internal/polymodel/polymodel.go, a SpecResult/TreeResult field) is the count of speculative KV positions to ROLL BACK after a speculative-decode verify pass — the drafted-but-rejected positions (== K - Accepted) fed to model.KVCache.Evict so the cache is bit-exact to never having drafted them. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictNode (radixkv policy)** - EvictNode (internal/radixkv/radixkv.go) is POLICY eviction in the radix prefix cache: it removes a node and its ENTIRE subtree regardless of LRU recency or lease state, returning tokens freed — the seam a quarantine verdict drives to purge a poisoned shared prefix. |
| o | defined | metric | evict | **Evictions (radixkv LRU counter)** - Evictions (internal/radixkv/radixkv.go Stats, surfaced via the agent KVMemoryStats) counts the LRU leaf evictions the radix prefix cache performed under its token budget — opportunistic drops of the least-recently-used cached prefix when LRUTokens exceeds the configured MaxTokens. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **Evicted (revocation count)** - evicted is the return count of internal/vdso VDSO.Revoke (and the Revocation/RevokeResponse.Evicted field in gateway/coherence.go) — how many pooled cache entries were causally dropped when an external trust witness was refuted, every entry admitted under that witness invalidated MESI-invalidate style. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **CanEvict** - CanEvict (internal/model/kvcache.go, internal/kvmmu) reports whether the KV cache is in a state where eviction may proceed. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **KVEvictionScope** - KVEvictionScope (internal/cachemeta/external_invalidation.go) is the closed scope type of an external KV eviction - exact span versus whole prefix cache. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **evict_manifest (provider action)** - evict_manifest is the vCache provider action that removes a warm row from the fak-owned local provider manifest without claiming a provider-side deletion. |
| o | defined | symbol | evict | **EvictionPolicy (radix-KV eviction policy)** - internal/agent/inkernel_planner.go (inKernelRadixEvictionPolicyFromEnv): the radixkv.EvictionPolicy selecting how the in-kernel radix KV cache evicts prefixes under pressure. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineCallsAvoided (tokendemo proof)** - The cmd/tokendemo served-read proof field (RawEngineCalls - FakEngineCalls) counting how many raw engine dispatches the kernel collapsed, alongside RawEngineCalls (the calls a stateless baseline would make), FakEngineCalls (the calls that actually ran the engine), EngineRanFak (the per-row flag that a call hit the real fak engine rather than the vDSO), and EngineDelayMs (the synthetic per-engine-call latency knob the timing proof injects). |

| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EvVDSOHit (vDSO event)** - The abi.Event kind emitted when a tool call is served locally by the vDSO fast path (no adjudication, By="vdso"), counted distinctly by observers; it names the same fast-path serve that VDSOOffNet ablates away, that FromVDSOKey describes as a tier-2 tool-result cache entry, and that VDSOProxyFill warms from admitted inbound tool_result blocks. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **glmDsaMatKernel (GLM-DSA compute selector)** - The internal/model Session method (moe_offload.go) that selects which matKernel the GLM-DSA forward runs its dense GEMMs through in one place, where the underlying compute knobs include FAK_QKERNEL (the SIMD quant-GEMM tier pin), LinearConvKernelDim (the Gated-DeltaNet short-conv kernel width), the CUDA pure kernels in cuda_kernels.cu, and the worktype KernelOptimization throughput program. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_operation_duration_seconds** - The Prometheus histogram of gateway operation latency labeled by operation type (adjudicate, syscall, admit), verdict, and deciding adjudicator, whose bucket cutoffs come from the package-level gatewayLatencyBuckets slice. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_kv_prefix_* (KV-prefix reuse family)** - The gateway's witnessed KV-prefix reuse metric family (fak_gateway_kv_prefix_turns_total, _prompt_tokens_total, _reuse_ratio, _turns_by_regime_total) that measures realized RadixAttention prompt-prefix reuse per in-kernel turn, with fak_gateway_kv_prefix_reused_tokens_total as the specific counter of prefix tokens the kernel did NOT re-prefill. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **routeEngine (per-call engine router)** - The gateway Server method that consults the optional per-call routing policy on every buildCall and returns the engine id to stamp onto abi.ToolCall.Engine (returning "" for the kernel-default path or an unset ensemble). |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineIDs (registry listing)** - abi.EngineIDs() returns the id-sorted list of registered inference-engine drivers from the engine registry snapshot, used for forensics, gateway validation, and engine selection. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **SGLangEngineID (sglang adapter)** - The internal/engine SGLang adapter registered under the engine id "sglang" (SGLangEngineID), a lifecycle-capable EngineDriver in the dispatchable registry; the external vLLM/SGLang serve harness is configured separately through the ENGINE_ARGS env var passed to the serve script. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **PureFakKernel (GCP plan test)** - The cmd/fak test TestClaudeGLMGCPA100PlanWiresPureFakKernel, asserting the Claude-GLM-on-GCP-datacenter GPU plan wires the pure in-kernel fak model path (the DRY cuda-binary subcommand) rather than an external upstream. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **normalizeTUIAgentGatewayURL (URL normalizer)** - The cmd/fak helper that validates and normalizes the gateway base URL a TUI agent / info command is pointed at, returning the canonical base used to build debug and agent requests. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **HTTPEngine (deleted OpenAI seam)** - The degenerate, never-wired engine.HTTPEngine OpenAI-client seam that was DELETED in v0.7.1 (with an architest invariant keeping the OpenAI client singular), leaving agent.HTTPPlanner as the one OpenAI client. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **enginedelay (gateway-engine identifier)** - enginedelay is the concrete gateway-engine identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring gateway-engine concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **externalengine (gateway-engine identifier)** - externalengine is the concrete gateway-engine identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring gateway-engine concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **externalengineobserved (gateway-engine identifier)** - externalengineobserved is the concrete gateway-engine identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring gateway-engine concepts. |

| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **kernelkv (gateway-engine identifier)** - kernelkv is the concrete gateway-engine identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring gateway-engine concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **kernelkvevents (gateway-engine identifier)** - kernelkvevents is the concrete gateway-engine identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring gateway-engine concepts. |

| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_tool_defer_cold_total (cold tool definitions deferred)** - internal/gateway/metrics_render.go: the cumulative count of COLD tool DEFINITIONS marked defer_loading:true on the outbound Anthropic body across a session - the 10x floor lever (--defer-cold-tools, #3232). It is cache-safe by construction (deterministic, byte-stable tools[] turn over turn), and the provider-side context drop it drives is OBSERVED via input_tokens/cache_read, never claimed by this counter. |
