| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_vdso_* metric family** - The Prometheus metric-name NAMESPACE prefix grouping every vDSO-specific series (fak_vdso_lookups_total, fak_vdso_hits_total, fak_vdso_hit_rate, fak_vdso_cache_fills_total, fak_vdso_invalidations_total, fak_vdso_misses_total) rendered by writeVDSOMetrics, kept alongside the fak_engine_cache_* and fak_cache_* families on /metrics. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **filterGatewayUsageSince (date filter)** - A cmd/fak helper (cachevalue_report.go / cachevalue.go / cachevalue_status.go) that drops durable gateway-usage ledger rows dated before a caller-supplied `since` cutoff, using gatewayUsageRowDate to parse each row's date. |
| o | defined | config | gateway-engine | **gateway_attributed (cache-tier provenance)** - The CacheCreationTierProvenanceGatewayAttributed constant value ("gateway_attributed") stamped on a Track-2 row's CacheCreationTierProvenance field to mark its CacheCreationTokensUpgraded split as fak's own per-turn upgrade witness (#2179), not a provider-reported 5m/1h split. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **GatewayChecked (preflight bit)** - A PreflightProbe bool field (internal/livecodebench and internal/terminalbench) recording whether the benchmark preflight even attempted the fak-gateway reachability probe, gating whether GatewayReachable's value can be trusted. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **GatewayReachable (preflight/probe bit)** - A liveness READ over the fak gateway's TCP listener: the nightrun package var gatewayReachable dials "host:port" with a short timeout, and the same-named PreflightProbe.GatewayReachable field in livecodebench/terminalbench preflights records that a `fak_gateway_reachable` gate passed. |
| o | defined | config | gateway-engine | **GATEWAY_UNREACHABLE (readiness status)** - The internal/dgxbridge LabGatewayUnreachable status string ("GATEWAY_UNREACHABLE"), set by NewLabReadiness when a lab-readiness probe's GatewayError bit is true, driving the GPU server lab-dispatch gate's next_action to "recover-private-gateway". |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **GatewayUsage (audit input)** - The AuditReportInput.GatewayUsage field (internal/auditusage) holding a GatewayUsageInput - the audit shell's own read of docs/nightrun/gateway-usage.jsonl (Path/Present/Rows, no chain-of-custody), folded by foldGateway into the report's GatewayRollup. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **inKernelOOMSnapshotData (debug rows)** - A gatewayMetrics method (internal/gateway/metrics_snapshot.go) that builds the []inKernelOOMSnapshot rows consumed by both the /debug/vars OOM block and the /metrics renderer, folding the live in-kernel OOM accumulator into a stable, serializable snapshot. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **KernelCPUPercent (harness axis)** - A gateway.SessionHarness field (json kernel_cpu_percent) carrying the harnessres.Half.CPUPercentAvg() reading for the harness's "kernel" half - the guard + in-process gateway PID sampled continuously - exposed live on /debug/vars and via guard_endpoints.go's guardHarnessToSession. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **KernelCPUSeconds (harness axis)** - A gateway.SessionHarness field (json kernel_cpu_seconds) carrying harnessres.Half.CPUSeconds() (user+system time) for the harness's guard+in-process-gateway "kernel" half, read by guardHarnessToSession when k.HaveCPU is set. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **KernelIOReadBytes (harness axis)** - A gateway.SessionHarness field (json kernel_io_read_bytes) carrying harnessres.Half.IOReadBytes for the guard+in-process-gateway "kernel" half, populated by guardHarnessToSession when k.HaveIO is set and rendered live at /debug/vars and in the guard exit summary (guard_endpoints.go, info_endpoints.go). |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **KernelIOWriteBytes (harness axis)** - A gateway.SessionHarness field (json kernel_io_write_bytes) carrying harnessres.Half.IOWriteBytes for the guard+in-process-gateway "kernel" half, populated by guardHarnessToSession when k.HaveIO is set alongside KernelIOReadBytes. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **KernelRSSBytes (harness axis)** - A gateway.SessionHarness field (json kernel_rss_bytes) carrying harnessres.Half.RSSBytes - the live resident-set-size reading for the guard+in-process-gateway "kernel" half - populated by guardHarnessToSession when k.HaveRSS is set and rendered via guardInfoBytesText in the guard's live status line. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **logGatewayOperation (per-call metrics)** - A gateway.Server method (internal/gateway/metrics_http.go, called from gateway.go's adjudicate/syscall paths and gateway_admit.go) that records ONE gateway operation's outcome inline into the live fak_gateway_operation_duration_seconds/fak_gateway_operations_total counters at call time. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **q8DecodeKernel (witness)** - The build-agnostic witness function (model.Q8DecodeKernel / internal q8DecodeKernel) that reports which Q8_0 decode inner-kernel tier this host resolved and whether the fused fast-decode GEMV is the active path, answering 'is the SIMD decode lane engaged?' at the /metrics surface. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_tool_defer (metric family)** - The Prometheus metric-name prefix grouping the cold-tool-deferral counters (fak_gateway_tool_defer_cold_total, fak_gateway_tool_defer_turns_total) the gateway emits when --defer-cold-tools marks cold tool definitions defer_loading on the outbound body. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **GatewayJudgeClient (trajctl)** - The trajctl JudgeClient implementation (internal/trajctl/judgeclient.go) backed by an OpenAI-compatible chat gateway, used by the rubric scorer to obtain an LLM verdict on a trajectory segment over HTTP. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **KernelCheck (dispatchtick preflight)** - The dispatchtick preflight struct (internal/dispatchtick/preflight.go) that captures the DOS kernel loop liveness probe, recording whether `dos loop --json` reports a live, admitting loop behind the dispatch preflight's kernel field. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **DemoEngine (quality runner)** - The quality regression-bisect helper (internal/quality/runner.go) that returns a ScriptedRunner engine for the demo case with an optional injected defect, exercising the expected-gate path via a scripted engine rather than a real inference backend. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **GatewaySteer (trajctl actuator)** - The trajctl steer actuator constructor (internal/trajctl/steer.go) that returns a SteerFunc POSTing a steer packet to an existing OpenAI-compatible gateway's /v1/fak/session/{id}/steer endpoint, the production wiring for delivering trajectory-control nudges. |
| o | defined | doc-term | gateway-engine | **LiveKernelEvent (WER hostfault)** - The Windows Error Reporting event class (WER 1001 LiveKernelEvent) that internal/hostfault classifies as a GPU driver live-kernel/TDR watchdog event, carrying a video-TDR bucket-141 or vendor watchdog dump signal, to gate the GPU_LIVEKERNEL hostfault class. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **matKernel (compute)** - The internal/model interface that selects which arithmetic a single decode block's weight matmuls run through — prep turns a normed activation into the kernel's operand form once, and mul applies a named weight to it — so the f32 and Q8 decode blocks share one block skeleton. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **f32Kernel (compute)** - The matKernel that runs a decode block fully in f32 — prep is the identity and every matmul routes through parMatRows (the in-order row-parallel GEMV), preserving the bit-exact reduction order. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **q8Kernel (compute)** - The matKernel that runs a decode block in Q8_0, quantizing the activation once per prep into a q8Vec and multiplying it against the prebuilt Q8_0 weight via qMatRows. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **q4Kernel (compute)** - The matKernel that runs a decode block in int4 (Q4): prep is the identity and mul applies the int4-packed weight via the q4 GEMV. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **residentKernel (compute)** - The cacheless Forward matKernel that prefers the exact f32 tensor when present and otherwise reads the quant-on-load Q8_0 resident copy via residentMatRows, keeping ordinary Forward bit-identical. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **backendKernel (compute)** - The matKernel that routes the GLM-MoE-DSA forward's dense GEMMs through a compute.Backend (the GPU pure kernels) instead of host residentMatRows, uploading weight+activation, running be.MatMul, and reading the result back. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **splitKernel (compute)** - A thin matKernel that dispatches each named matmul per-weight to a host sub-kernel or a device sub-kernel by a predicate, implementing the --n-cpu-moe split (offloaded experts on host RAM, dense projections/router/attention on GPU). |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **dsaIndexKernel (compute)** - The optional device-path interface for GLM-MoE-DSA's learned-indexer score + top-k selection (indexSelect), taken only when the backendKernel's compute.Backend advertises DSAIndexBackend; otherwise the host f64 score/top-k loop runs. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **dsaSparseKernel (compute)** - The optional device-path interface for GLM-MoE-DSA's sparse-attention compute (sparseAttend: per-head softmax(scale·q·k)·V over the host-selected keys), taken only when the backend advertises DSASparseBackend. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **KernelCounters (turnbench)** - The turnbench replay harness's snapshot struct of the coordinator kernel's call-path tallies (submits, vDSO hits, engine calls, denies, transforms) gathered over a replayed trace. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **FromKernel (toollint)** - toollint.FromKernel builds tool-lint facts (fast-path tables + preflight schemas) from the process-global kernel registries (vdso.Default and the engine registries) for the registry-only lint view. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **inkernel (engine id)** - The id under which internal/modelengine registers internal/model as an abi.EngineDriver, so `--engine inkernel` (or one blank-import) dispatches a tool call to the model fused into the fak process via a real Prefill+Step decode. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **InKernelPlanner** - The agent.Planner backed by the in-kernel model: one Complete runs a greedy Prefill+Step decode over a kernel-owned KV cache using the loaded model + tokenizer. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **InKernelCapacityError** - The request-time error the InKernelPlanner returns when a request's prompt + max-new tokens would exceed device capacity, refusing before any decode allocation. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **InKernelOOMError** - The agent-level, recovered form of an in-kernel device-allocation OOM during decode, carrying the failed byte count, memory class, and site. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **inKernelOOMObservation** - The gateway-side function that classifies an error as a recovered in-kernel OOM and extracts its (class, bytes, site) so the metrics layer can count it. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **InKernelOOMRetryStats** - The struct reporting idle-pool trim retries attempted after in-kernel device OOMs, bucketed by backend, memory class, and outcome, surfaced through the InKernelOOMRetryReporter interface. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **InKernelMemoryPressureTrimStats** - The struct reporting PROACTIVE idle-pool trims the in-kernel planner takes under memory pressure, in bounded-label rows per backend and memory class, surfaced via InKernelMemoryPressureTrimReporter. |
| o | defined | config | gateway-engine | **FAK_INKERNEL_KVMMU** - The environment-variable gate that opts the gateway into the in-kernel KV-MMU slot-reclaim bridge (a real SlotFreed -> KV eviction); default-off, where the bridge is an inert no-op. |
| o | defined | config | gateway-engine | **FAK_XENGINE_KV** - The environment-variable gate that opts an Arena into becoming the cross-engine zero-copy KV co-residence RegionBackend (the xenginekv package, #448); unset leaves blob as the default. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineID (registry)** - The string id that keys an inference engine driver in the abi engine registry (e.g. bench.Options.EngineID, the const EngineID="inkernel"); New(engineID) binds a kernel to it. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **EngineCalls (counter)** - The coordinator-kernel counter (kernel.Counters.EngineCalls, mirrored into agent/gateway metrics) of tool-call reaps that actually dispatched to the engine rather than being served from the vDSO. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineModel (provenance)** - The model identifier the benchmark engine ran under, recorded in bench.Options.EngineModel and stamped into the report Provenance. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineRoute (modelroute)** - The modelroute.Target method returning the locality-leading route string (e.g. "local:..." / "anthropic:...") that the host stamps into abi.ToolCall.Engine for residency classification. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **RegisterEngine** - The abi registry function that adds an inference engine driver keyed by id (local/remote/multi); modelengine and others call it from init(). |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **RegisterReadEngine** - agent.RegisterReadEngine registers the working-tree-confined read engine under FakReadEngineID, so a cache-missed fak_read dispatches to a real confined disk read. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **FakReadEngineID** - The const engine id "fakread" that fak_read binds on its abi.ToolCall so the kernel routes the call to the confined read engine. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **LifecycleEngine** - The optional abi extension interface (admit -> per-step -> stream -> reclaim) an engine implements when it advertises EngineLifecycleCap, giving the gateway a streaming decode lifecycle. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineRequest** - One in-flight decode admitted to a LifecycleEngine; its Tokens() channel streams the decode steps. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineToken** - One streamed decode step from a LifecycleEngine — the generated token id plus optional bytes. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **EngineLifecycleCap** - The abi.Capability ("engine.lifecycle.v1") an engine advertises from Caps() when it implements LifecycleEngine, so a consumer can detect lifecycle support. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **gatewayMetrics (registry)** - The gateway's in-process metrics aggregator struct (constructed by newGatewayMetrics) that tallies HTTP, operation, inference, and in-kernel-OOM counters and renders the /metrics Prometheus exposition. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **GatewayURL (field)** - The gateway base URL a client is pointed at — e.g. the dropin child's loopback gateway_url and computeTarget.GatewayURL for a gateway-kind compute target. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **GatewayAddr (swebench)** - The fak gateway address (host:port, default localhost:8080) the swebench integrator uses to build a Planner against a running gateway. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **targetGatewayURL (compute kind)** - The compute-target kind constant ("gateway-url") in cmd/fak/computetarget.go selecting an existing fak/OpenAI-compatible gateway reached over HTTP as the compute backend. |
| o | defined | config | gateway-engine | **FAK_GATEWAY_KEY** - The environment variable holding the gateway bearer credential that fak CLI subcommands send when a gateway runs with --require-key. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **defaultGatewayBearerToken** - The cmd/fak helper that resolves the default bearer credential (from FAK_GATEWAY_KEY via the secret loader) used as the --key default across fak CLI subcommands. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **FakGateway (browseraction)** - The browseraction contract field naming the fak gateway base URL the browser-action agent is pointed at (e.g. http://localhost:8080/v1). |
| o | defined | doc-term | gateway-engine | **StatusBadGateway (HTTP 502)** - The net/http StatusBadGateway (502) constant the gateway returns when an upstream provider is unreachable, returns an unrecognized tool-call format, or fails cache invalidation. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_up** - The Prometheus gauge that is 1 when the fak gateway process is scrapeable. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_build_info** - The Prometheus gauge (value 1) carrying static fak gateway build/runtime labels (version, engine, model, vdso). |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_http_requests_total** - The Prometheus counter of HTTP requests the gateway served, labeled by route, method, and status. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_http_request_duration_seconds** - The Prometheus histogram of gateway HTTP request latency, labeled by route, method, and status. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_operations_total** - The Prometheus counter of gateway kernel operations labeled by operation, verdict, reason, disposition, and deciding adjudicator (by). |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_inflight_requests** - The Prometheus gauge of HTTP requests currently executing inside the gateway. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_time_to_ready_seconds** - The Prometheus gauge of total wall-clock from process start to the gateway becoming ready to serve (0 until ready). |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_startup_phase_duration_seconds** - The Prometheus gauge of each named gateway boot phase's wall-clock cost (flag-parse, policy-load, planner-init, vdso-config, kernel-init, listener-bind, model-load). |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_gateway_startup_unaccounted_seconds** - The Prometheus gauge of boot wall-clock not explained by any named startup phase (time_to_ready minus the sum of phase durations); near-zero means startup is fully instrumented. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **fak_kernel_decisions_total** - The Prometheus counter of the gateway's passive rung-decision distribution (rungObs), labeled by rung, kind, and reason. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **VDSOHits (counter)** - The coordinator-kernel counter of tool calls served from the vDSO fast path without dispatching to the engine. |
| o | defined | metric | gateway-engine | **VDSOHitRate (ratio)** - The fraction of kernel submissions served by the vDSO fast path (VDSOHits/Calls), surfaced as bench.Report.VDSOHitRate and the fak_gateway_vdso_hit_ratio / fak_vdso_hit_rate gauges. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **SetVDSO** - The Kernel method that toggles the vDSO fast path on/off (the --vdso ablation, unit 5); off makes every call fall through to adjudication + engine. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **VDSOEnabled** - The Kernel method reporting whether the vDSO fast path is currently active. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **vdsoOff (ablation)** - The Kernel field / `--vdso=off` ablation flag that, when set, makes Submit skip the FastPath entirely — the 'off' arm of the A/B benchmark. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **vdsoOn (arm)** - The vDSO-enabled benchmark arm — the RunArm vdsoOn=true call labeled "vdso_on" that serves repeats from the fast path. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **VDSOPure (tier-1)** - The turnbench ClassBreakdown count of tier-1 'pure local serve' vDSO hits (e.g. deterministic calculate calls). |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **VDSODedup (tier-2)** - The turnbench ClassBreakdown count of tier-2 vDSO hits served from the content cache on a duplicate read (the 'vdso_tier2' source). |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **VDSOStatic (tier-3)** - The turnbench ClassBreakdown count of tier-3 'static local serve' vDSO hits (e.g. list_all_airports). |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **NewSchedulingGateway (slot-scheduling decorator)** - NewSchedulingGateway(gw, sched) (internal/microagent/slotsched.go) wraps the ONE shared model Gateway with cooperative slot scheduling, so every model call routes through the Scheduler with a priority hook before it reaches the underlying gateway. |
| o | defined | subsystem | gateway-engine | **modelengine** - The binding that wires the in-kernel model into the kernel as a registered EngineDriver under the id 'inkernel'. |
| o | defined | subsystem | gateway-engine | **modelroute** - The model-routing policy spine: a version-tagged JSON manifest that classifies a tool call by aspect into a plan for which model(s) serve each aspect. |
| o | defined | subsystem | gateway-engine | **xenginekv** - The cross-engine zero-copy KV co-residence seam: a RegionBackend that maps an external engine's KV cache and fak's args/results into one shared addressable arena. |
| o | defined | config | gateway-engine | **DefaultGatewayBaseURL** - DefaultGatewayBaseURL (internal/frontierswe/env_adapter.go) is the default OpenAI-compatible fak gateway base URL (http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1) the SWE-bench env adapter wires the shim to. |
| o | defined | config | gateway-engine | **GatewayBaseURL** - GatewayBaseURL (frontierswe EnvAdapterConfig; cmd/fak swebench_fleet gatewayBaseURL) carries the fak gateway base URL a run should point its client at. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **gatewayBase** - gatewayBase (internal/frontierswe/env_adapter.go) is the resolved gateway base URL variable threaded through env-adapter boundary validation and job YAML. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **engineeringWorkKinds** - engineeringWorkKinds (internal/fleetaccounts/route.go) is the set of work kinds routed to engineering seats. |
| o | defined | subsystem | gateway-engine | **gatewayusageledger** - Package gatewayusageledger (internal/gatewayusageledger) persists per-turn gateway usage rows that the audit-usage fold reads. |
| o | defined | symbol | gateway-engine | **matchKernelGlob** - matchKernelGlob (internal/hooks/gate_priorart.go, cmd/fak/sota.go) matches a path against a kernel-surface glob for the prior-art gate and `fak sota`. |

| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **GatedScoreAntiCheat (anti-cheat score zeroing)** - The internal/frontierswe function that applies anti-cheat zeroing on top of GatedScore: a trial flagged in scoring/anticheat.json scores 0 regardless of its raw gated value (score.go). |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardInfoShortCount (guard-panel count formatter)** - The cmd/fak helper that renders an integer compactly for a budget cell in the guard info panel (950 -> '950', larger values abbreviated) so a count fits its column. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **guard_allow_scope.go (allow-overlay scope ladder)** - cmd/fak/guard_allow_scope.go (#5180, epic #5170): the file that defines the guard allow-overlay SCOPE ladder - repo < user < env < session - and the rule that a narrower, more-ephemeral scope layers on top and therefore wins. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardAllowScopeRank (scope precedence rank)** - cmd/fak/guard_allow_scope.go: maps a scope name to its integer precedence rank - higher rank = narrower and more ephemeral = layered on top; an unknown scope ranks below everything so it can never claim to shadow a known scope. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardAllowScopeSession (session scope name const)** - cmd/fak/guard_allow_scope.go: the string constant "session" naming the session-scope overlay layer; the repo/user/env layer names come from guardAllowOverlayPaths, so this is the one scope name that needs its own constant. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardAllowScopeDurabilityNote (scope durability legend)** - cmd/fak/guard_allow_scope.go: returns the one-clause operator legend saying whether a widening recorded at a given scope survives the next launch, so every scope listing answers the durability question without the reader inferring it from a path. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardAllowLayersWithSessionScope (session layer appender)** - cmd/fak/guard_allow_scope.go: appends the session-scope layer AFTER the base overlay layers, leaving the base order untouched, so the session scope is applied last and therefore takes precedence. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **FAK_GUARD_SESSION_ID (guard session-id env)** - cmd/fak/guard_allow_scope.go: the environment variable (constant guardAllowSessionIDEnv) that carries the guard session id to a process that did not resolve it itself, so operator tooling beside a guarded session can address that session's scope. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **FAK_GUARD_STOPS_LEDGER (stops-ledger path env)** - cmd/fak/guard_stops.go: the environment variable whose VALUE is the absolute stops-ledger path the guard installer injects into the Stop hook child; unset means recording is disabled entirely. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardStopsLedgerEnv (stops-ledger env constant)** - cmd/fak/guard_stops.go: the Go constant that holds the string "FAK_GUARD_STOPS_LEDGER" - the single in-code spelling of the stops-ledger env var, so the installer and the Stop hook can never disagree about the name. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **FLEET_MSG_GUARD (commit-message advisory mode env)** - cmd/fak/commit_msg_advisory.go: the gate-mode env var for the commit-message advisory; it can only SILENCE the advisory (`off`), never escalate it to a refusal. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **GATED_WIDEN (amendment class)** - internal/policy/amendment.go: the AmendmentClass constant for operator-gated LOOSENING of a policy knob (allow overlay, reload-widen) - the class that may move a knob outward, but only through an operator gate. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **guardCodexChatGPTBackendBaseURL (Codex backend base URL)** - cmd/fak/guard_codex_oauth.go: the constant base URL of the ChatGPT backend Codex API the guard's Codex OAuth path talks to. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **guardToolprocJournalRel (toolproc journal path)** - cmd/fak/guard_toolproc_hooks.go: the workspace-relative journal path (.fak/toolproc/journal.jsonl) the toolproc hooks append to - one shared journal per workspace, made absolute at install so the hook is cwd-independent. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **cmdGuardStdinInteractive (attended-STDIN probe)** - cmd/fak/guard_support.go: reports whether the guard's own STDIN is a real console, using term.IsTerminal rather than an os.ModeCharDevice test - because on Windows a redirected stdin reports AS a character device and a FileMode check would read the headless case as interactive. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardOwnsInteractiveTerminal (terminal-host ownership probe)** - cmd/fak/guard_resolve.go: reports whether this guard owns an interactive terminal host it may act in - false for any dispatcher-owned session (FAK_DISPATCH_ID, FAK_HEADLESS, CI), then per-OS on WT_SESSION / TERM_PROGRAM / TERM+SSH_TTY. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardInfoAgentText (info-panel agent cell)** - cmd/fak/info_panels.go: renders the agent cell of one `fak info` guard-session row; it deliberately does NOT render Recontinue as sub-agent depth, because Recontinue is the same agent after a budget-reset re-continuation, not a spawned child. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardNegframeBegin (SessionStart negframe row)** - cmd/fak/guard_negframe_summary.go: records the SessionStart negframe row and TRUNCATES the journal first, because SessionStart is the session boundary - without the reset the exit summary would report a lifetime total under a per-session label. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardNegframeReframe (gated emit-time reframe)** - cmd/fak/guard_negframe_summary.go: the single gated seam that reframes fak-authored fragments at emit time - on the treatment arm it reframes and reports that pass's telemetry, on the control arm it concatenates the fragments unreframed; non-fak-authored fragments survive byte-for-byte on both arms. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardNegframeSummary (exit-summary negframe fold)** - cmd/fak/guard_negframe_summary.go: folds the workspace negframe journal that the SessionStart reframe feeds into the one-line guard exit summary. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **maybeRecordGuardSessionIndex (guarded index append)** - cmd/fak/guard_sessions.go: the precondition wrapper around recordGuardSessionIndex - it returns "" without touching the index when there is no audit journal, no command or no trace id, so a launch with nothing to publish never appends a partial row. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **recordGuardUsage (guard exit usage row)** - cmd/fak/usagelog_record.go: appends the single best-effort usage row for a `fak guard` invocation at exit, once (sync.Once) and only when a start time was stamped; any failure is swallowed so meta-observability can never change the verb's outcome. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **guard_policy.go (guard policy verb router)** - cmd/fak/guard_policy.go (#5424, epic #5170): the router for `fak guard policy <verb>` - it holds the verb table and dispatches to the explain / diff implementations that live in their own files. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **floorgate.go (tool-schema floor-growth gate)** - internal/mcpfootprint/floorgate.go (#2924): the one-way ratchet on fak's always-sent MCP tool-schema floor - it refuses GROWTH past FloorBudgetTokens (FLOOR_BUDGET_EXCEEDED) and also refuses SLACK once a deferral lever has driven the floor well below the ceiling (FLOOR_BUDGET_STALE), so the budget must be re-pinned. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **batchedGatedMLP (batched FFN gated-MLP)** - internal/model/arch.go: runs one layer's f32 gated MLP over N already-post-normed rows and returns the [N,H] down projection - gate/up matMulBatch, then fuseGatedMLPPanels, then down. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **fuseGatedMLPPanels (in-place gate/up panel fuse)** - internal/model/arch.go: finishes a batched gated MLP's gate and up panels IN PLACE - the single source of truth for that mechanical axis, so every prefill/quant/batch twin applies it identically. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **appendGrowthgateReapLedger (reap accountability trail)** - cmd/fak/growthgate.go: appends one JSON line per growth-gate reap decision - the accountability trail for BOTH a dry run and an --apply run, mirroring the fleet-janitor ledger. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **codexLoopGate (codex-loop launch gate)** - The pre-launch gate for Codex workers (on `fak codex`/`fak guard` and the dispatch tick): before spawning a Codex child it audits recent Codex session transcripts (the newest `limit` sessions modified within `since-hours`) plus the current thread, and refuses when a session is diagnosed a repetition LOOP (repeated tool outputs) or unproductive ACTION churn at or above the configured severity threshold (loop|action|off), or when the current thread bypassed fak guard (unguarded). Its limit / since-hours / threshold defaults and diagnosis reason-codes live under this name. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **guardInfo panel (guard status overlay)** - The live `fak info` / `fak guard --split` status overlay: the panels enumerated by guardInfoPanels() (agents summary, endpoints, cache hit-frac/pct, incident text, legend, trend sparkline, turns-saved, hook-latency line), fitted to the pane height by composeGuardInfoPanels and drawn by renderGuardInfoInteractiveBlock / runGuardInfoOverlay from a /debug/vars snapshot. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **guard park budget (guardParkBudget)** - guardParkBudget() resolves the shared guard-park ceiling from FAK_GUARD_PARK_BUDGET (a Go duration, default 24h, clamped to [0,24h]; 0 restores the immediate fail-loud refusal) bounding how long ANY guard park may block - chiefly the STALE_CRED re-login park guardParkForRelogin, which waits for an expired credential to be refreshed at credPath before resuming, and which the separate spend-cap park reuses as its clamp. |
| o | defined | concept | guard-gate | **guard auth-crash recovery (guardMaybeRecoverAuthCrash)** - Detects that the wrapped agent exited non-zero because its subscription token expired mid-session, polls the pinned credential file for a recovery window, and - if a fresh login lands - auto-relaunches the SAME command with the agent's resume flag (--continue) appended, so the guarded session self-heals instead of needing a manual re-run. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardRecoveryPrompt (refusal carry-forward prompt)** - Builds the one-shot prompt the gateway injects into a resumed guarded run to carry forward the PRIOR run's capability-floor refusals ('the previous guarded run recorded capability-floor refusal(s) ... do not re-propose the same refused call unchanged'), assembled from the []guardRefusalCarry items read out of the audit journal. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guard child supervisor entry (runGuardChild*)** - The entry points that spawn the guarded child under supervision and report its outcome (runGuardChildAndReport and its supervised variant). |
| o | defined | concept | guard-gate | **guard allow-overlay (guardApplyAllowOverlay)** - The operator-authored allow-list overlay behind `fak guard allow`: guardAllowOverlayPath resolves the file (env override, else the repo default), loadGuardAllowOverlay reads and validates it, and guardApplyAllowOverlay unions its allow / allow_prefix entries into the runtime capability floor at launch so a tool the base floor never enumerated stops hitting DEFAULT_DENY. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **guard API-key env (FAK_GUARD_API_KEY_ENV)** - FAK_GUARD_API_KEY_ENV (Go const fleetGuardAPIKeyEnvEnv) is the fleet-launcher env var whose VALUE names the env var (e.g. ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) that actually holds the upstream provider API key; the launchers read it and splice `--api-key-env <name>` into the guard child's argv, opting into API-key billing so guard's managed-cache AUTO resolves ACTIVE instead of the passive subscription-OAuth default. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **guard stop-hook config (installGuardStopHook)** - The install-and-tune surface for the guard's Claude Stop-hook that auto-continues past deny-all turns: installGuardStopHook wires the hook into the session --settings, guardStopHookDefaultWarn (=3) is the default warn rung of the warn/final/max ladder, and fak_guard_deny_all_consecutive is the gateway GAUGE the hook polls for the current consecutive-deny-all count. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **repo-guard env knobs (FAK_REPO_GUARD / _SEVERITY)** - The two env knobs that tune repo-guard: FAK_REPO_GUARD (master switch - `off` disables every rung, `warn` caps all rungs to advisory) and FAK_REPO_GUARD_SEVERITY (per-reason dial setting each reason to off/record/warn/deny). |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **PreflightGate (rehearsal host-capability gate)** - One host capability a benchmark rehearsal needs, as a {Name, OK, Detail} row (internal/terminalbench, internal/livecodebench): the slice of these is RehearsalPreflight.Gates, and any not-OK row lands in BlockingReasons so the harness refuses to claim a result on an unready host. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **gate backpressure (ApplyGateBackpressure / REFUSE_GATE)** - The fifth (backpressure) admission term of the fleet spawn preflight (internal/dispatchtick): a pure fold that reads MEASURED guard-hook gate-latency health and, when the rollup is breached, LOWERS the effective worker cap (never raises it) - holding a WARM fleet at its live count and refusing with the closed PreflightRefuseGate = "REFUSE_GATE" verdict carrying the GATE_PRESSURE token, while a COLD fleet is held to DefaultGateMinWorkers so a slow kernel cannot deadlock itself. |
| o | defined | concept | guard-gate | **guard account rotation / failover (guardRotate)** - The two paths that move a guarded run onto a different on-box Claude account: guardRotateOffCooldown (internal/guardrotate) rotates off a rate-limited seat BEFORE launch by consulting the fleet-shared cooldown store, and the accountFailover type behind gateway.AccountFailoverFunc supplies a REPLACEMENT upstream credential MID-session when the active seat is walled. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardEndpoints (session endpoints provider)** - The session-endpoints provider `fak guard` hands the gateway (newGuardEndpointsProvider / guardEndpointNodes): it assembles a gateway.SessionEndpoints of the on-box Claude seat roster (active / walled marks, re-pulled each /debug/vars so a mid-session failover stays live) plus the kernel and serving nodes, purely as display metadata for the `fak info` status area - never a token. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardLoopbackOnly (loopback-bind predicate)** - The predicate guardLoopbackOnly(addr) reporting whether a listen address binds ONLY the loopback interface; it mirrors the gateway's own unexported loopbackOnly so guard can re-assert the no-auth warning the gateway skips when handed a pre-bound listener. A bare ":port" (all interfaces) and any routable IP are NOT loopback. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guard report layout (guardRow / guardSection)** - The text-report building blocks of a guard decision summary: a titled section, its rows, and free-form notes. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **guard sessions index (guardSessions)** - `fak guard sessions`, the operator query surface over the durable local guard-session INDEX (internal/guardsessions): every `fak guard` launch appends one JSONL row, and the verb lists recorded sessions newest-first or resolves a short prefix of the handle OR the guard trace id to the single session it names. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guard replay / harness-profile tests** - The two guard test files that lock guard behavior against drift: cmd/fak/guard_replay_test.go (TestGuardReplay* drives runGuardReplay over recorded trace fixtures end-to-end) and cmd/fak/guard_harness_profiles_test.go (TestHarnessProfileFloorCoverage asserts the shipped floor admits every first-class harness profile's required tools). |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **tuiGuardSchema (guard pane schema tag)** - The versioned schema-identifier constant tuiGuardSchema = "fak.tui.guard.v1" that tags the guard pane's data-model version: it is passed as the Pane.Schema in the TUI plugin registry and stamped on every emitted tuiGuardReport. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **ActionGated (provider-cache action state)** - ActionGated is one of the three ProviderActionState values ("gated") in internal/vcacheobserve: it marks a provider-cache action-plan row (heartbeat_pin, explicit_cache) that cannot be spent yet because it still lacks the required warm-transport or byte-identical-prefix witness, so apply refuses it as RefusedGated. |
| o | defined | concept | guard-gate | **advisoryGate (advisory report gate)** - trendreport.AdvisoryGate (reused by cadencereport and milestonereport CheckGate) is the shared advisory CI gate over a folded report: it PASSES (exit 0, '<LABEL> OK') every quality finding, including score and climb-regression advisories, because a trend report is a mirror rather than a second quality gate - and it FAILS (exit 1, '<LABEL> INCOMPLETE') only when a dimension could not be measured. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **gateGodFile (god-file / god-function growth ratchet)** - gateGodFileGrowthTree (internal/hooks/gate_godfile.go) is the size ratchet run by `fak hygiene` (pre-push) and `make ci`: it refuses any tracked non-test .go file over the 1500-line ceiling (held aligned with the file-only sibling gate internal/godfileceiling) and any function over the 400-line ceiling, except entries grandfathered at their frozen size in the god-file baseline, which may grow within a bounded 20% slack band before the gate re-engages (the frozen anchor itself only ratchets down on regen). |
| o | defined | metric | guard-gate | **gateStats (headroom compression counters)** - The lifetime counters of the headroom result-compression gate (headroom.Stats): how many tool results were Considered, how many Compressed, the BytesIn / BytesOut of those compressions, and the per-reason breakdown of results left uncompressed (skipped empty / poison / no-saving / not-worth); snapshotted via Gate.Stats() and surfaced in `fak headroom status` and cachevalue status. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **gateTierDeclared (leaf tier-declaration gate)** - The gate that refuses a new leaf / package unless its architest tier is declared. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **gateVerdict (advisory-gate decision)** - trendreport.GateVerdict is one advisory-gate decision - a process exit code plus a human message produced by AdvisoryGate - that each report's CheckGate returns and Envelope.WithGate folds into the cadence and milestone report envelopes, reconciling to OK (exit 0) or ACTION / INCOMPLETE (exit 1). |
| o | defined | cli-verb | guard-gate | **growthGate (append-only bloat ratchet)** - The `fak growthgate` unbounded-growth ratchet: it censuses append-only ledger/log files (.jsonl/.log/.err) across one or more repo roots, classifies each against fixed per-class BYTE budgets (internal/growthgate.Classify), reports an OK/ACTION verdict, and can --reap COLD, over-budget, disposable logs. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **guardFleet (cross-machine fleet pane provider)** - The display-only, cross-MACHINE fleet-pane provider `fak guard` installs into the gateway (SetSessionFleetProvider): behind a 30s TTL cache it folds published per-machine snapshots (optionally unioned with live LAN spine peers) into a fleetpane.FleetDoc so `fak info` and /debug/vars can render fleet-of-machines health - machine ids, states, per-machine session counts and rolled-up totals. |
| o | defined | concept | guard-gate | **hygiene gates (whole-tree audit gates)** - The whole-tree audit gates run in one process by `fak hygiene` (HygieneGates() in internal/hooks/tree.go): the family of `--audit-tree` checks - DOC_PLACEMENT, BROKEN_LINK, SECRET_SHAPE, INDEX_SYNC, GOD_FILE_GROWTH, BARE_DEV_SPELLING and others - that `make hygiene` / `make ci` enforce over `git ls-files` as a pre-push backstop. |
| o | defined | cli-verb | guard-gate | **guard RSI CLI verbs (cmdGuardRsiScorecard)** - The `fak` CLI verbs that run the guard-RSI scorecard and the guard-verdict RSI fold. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **prefixGuard (cache-ablation prefix-stability feature)** - FeaturePrefixGuard = "prefix_guard" (internal/ablate) is a lossless provider-prompt-cache-control feature in the cache-ablation sweep vocabulary: it guards the STABILITY of the cacheable prompt prefix so provider prompt-cache economics can be relied upon, and when swept on it only bumps a simulated reused-token counter. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardSessionCardHandle (live Slack session card)** - The process-wide handle to a guard session's live-updating Slack root card: a guard process runs exactly one session, so a single package-level var is set right after the session root is queued (newGuardSessionCard) and its terminal outcome edit is written from finishGuardChildAndReport. It stays nil - every method a no-op - whenever Slack is unconfigured or the root did not queue, so neither launch nor teardown branches on Slack. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **reviewGate (RSI review-fire value gate)** - The value-gate on the periodic self-improvement review fire (internal/rsiloop): it estimates the expected value of forking a 'review this session for extractable skill/memory' turn (session novelty, unseen tool sequences, error density) in token-equivalents, prices the fork's COST on the same compaction-economics basis (a re-read prefix billed at the cache-read marginal plus generated output), spawns ONLY when expected value exceeds cost, and self-tunes the threshold from the realized outcomes of prior fires. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **RetryGate** - RetryGate (internal/resume/outcome.go) decides from attempt history, the latest outcome, and a max-attempts cap whether another resume attempt is allowed. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **TokenRateGate** - TokenRateGate (internal/gateway/token_admission.go) is the admission gate that reserves estimated token usage against a token-rate policy before a turn runs. |
| o | defined | subsystem | guard-gate | **memgate** - Package memgate (internal/memgate) checks whether a heavy model load should proceed under current memory pressure. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **GateClass** - GateClass (internal/callavoid/classgate.go) decides from class-memo input whether a model call can be avoided (ClassGateDecision). |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **gate_commitmsg** - gate_commitmsg.go (internal/hooks) is the COMMIT_MSG hook gate, a Go port of check_commit_msg.py, that lints commit subjects before they land. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **GateDisposition** - GateDisposition (internal/ctxmmu/disposition.go) grades an observed context disposition against the wanted disposition, given evidence, returning a DispositionOutcome. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **GateEphemeral** - GateEphemeral (internal/ctxmmu/ephemeral_gate.go) decides whether ephemeral text may be reclassified, returning an EphemeralGateOutcome. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **gatePriorArt** - gatePriorArt (internal/hooks/gate_priorart.go) is the staged-diff hook gate that emits prior-art findings for changes matching kernel globs. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **GateReport** - GateReport (internal/vcacheqa and internal/vllmcompile) is the structured result a QA gate returns, with an OK() fold over its checks. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardIsCodex** - guardIsCodex (cmd/fak/guard_codex.go) detects whether the guarded child command is a Codex CLI invocation, selecting the Codex install shape. |
| o | defined | config | guard-gate | **guardDisabled** - guardDisabled (cmd/fak/dispatch_tick.go) reports whether dispatch must skip the fak guard wrapper (GUARD_DISABLED environment switch). |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **ScoreTrendGate** - ScoreTrendGate (internal/cachevalueledger/ledger.go) grades a ledger's score trend and gates on regression (TrendGateResult). |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **compute_gated_score** - compute_gated_score is the reference SWE-bench scoring rule (correctness-gated speedup) that internal/frontierswe/score.go's GatedScore ports to Go. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **finishGuardChildAndReport** - finishGuardChildAndReport (cmd/fak/guard_child.go) folds a guarded child's exit state, gateway shutdown, and audit summary into the final guard report. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guard_child** - guard_child.go (cmd/fak) resolves the guarded child's upstream wire/credential posture and runs the child process under the guard. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **formatGuardRefusalCarryForward** - formatGuardRefusalCarryForward (cmd/fak/guard_carryforward.go) formats the carried-forward guard refusals for the startup banner. |
| o | defined | symbol | guard-gate | **guardRefusalCarry** - guardRefusalCarry (cmd/fak/guard.go) is one carried-forward refusal record surfaced in the guard banner at next start. |

A base scoring method whose calibration drifts becomes a first-class trajctl Objective whose own W3 score is the calibration-delta (MetaScorerMethod="calibration-delta"), advancing from the baseline coefficient captured at declaration (MetaTarget). The one-level fence forbids anything from targeting the meta scorer itself (ErrMetaFence), enforced structurally at three places that cannot drift apart: MetaObjective construction, validateMetaTarget at the ledger boundary, and Calibrate skipping the meta method. |

Refused rather than falsely acked as a phantom 202, so the caller learns the input will not land. |

| o | defined | config | policy-capability | **posture enum value (per-subsystem stance)** - These are named stance constants on different subsystems' Posture enums: resume's PostureCold/PostureWarm/PostureUnknown classify whether a session's provider prefix is still cache-warm, compactcohere's PostureBlock is the PreCompact-hook stance that exits 2 to block harness auto-compaction, and adjudicator's PostureFailClosed (default-deny) and PostureAdmitAndLog (admit low-risk reads with forensic metadata) are the two tool-admission stances. |
| o | defined | symbol | policy-capability | **DispatchPreflight (dispatch admission)** - DispatchPreflight (cmd/fak) folds host safety, account availability, kernel capacity, and seat-pool checks (its PreflightInput) into a verdict on whether a new issue-resolution worker may safely spawn, with PreflightReady the ggufload load-time gate verdict that says a model header parsed and fits. |
| o | defined | symbol | policy-capability | **fetch_policy (benchmark refund-doc tool)** - fetch_policy (internal/agent toolFetchDoc) and its sibling read_refund_policy are the agent-benchmark tool names for fetching an airline refund document whose body carries an indirect prompt injection, the call the context-MMU quarantines at result admission to prove the floor. |
| o | defined | symbol | policy-capability | **TierPolicy (ctxresidency tier)** - TierPolicy (internal/ctxresidency) is the always-resident context-residency tier holding the versioned deny/allow and safety-critical floor spans, which are never paged out under budget pressure during context compression. |
| o | defined | symbol | policy-capability | **DefaultAdmissionPolicy (gateway scheduling)** - gateway.DefaultAdmissionPolicy returns the shipping AdmissionPolicy defaults for the request scheduler — a 256-sequence running cap, a token budget, a bounded waiting queue, and per-round aging — bounding how many sequences the engine admits concurrently. |

| o | defined | symbol | policy-capability | **PolicyFor (loopmgr governor)** - loopmgr.Policies.PolicyFor returns the effective loop-governor Policy for a given loop id — the per-loop override if one is registered, else the default loop policy (pause flags, max-consecutive-refusals, aging). |

| o | defined | symbol | pool | **poolkey (pool identifier)** - poolkey is the concrete pool identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring pool concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **gradedPool (evidence-graded candidate pool)** - cmd/fak/route_place_evidence.go: applies capability grades to a candidate pool. A grade that did not reach the bar leaves its candidate exactly as it was - ungraded and therefore top-rung only - because failing to MEASURE something is not evidence against it; a model claimed by both a hand declaration and evidence is refused by the caller before this runs. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **pool implementation surface for certpool** - `certpool` is a concrete tree symbol classified in the `pool` family. This row positions that implementation surface so lookup does not conflate it with the family umbrella. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **pool implementation surface for systemcertpool** - `systemcertpool` is a concrete tree symbol classified in the `pool` family. This row positions that implementation surface so lookup does not conflate it with the family umbrella. |

| o | defined | symbol | pool | **quotaNonPagedPoolUsage** - quotaNonPagedPoolUsage (internal/harnessres/sample_windows.go) is the PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS field: current non-paged-pool bytes charged to the sampled process. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **quotaPeakPagedPoolUsage** - quotaPeakPagedPoolUsage (internal/harnessres/sample_windows.go) is the PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS field: peak paged-pool bytes the sampled process ever charged. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **quotaPeakNonPagedPoolUsage** - quotaPeakNonPagedPoolUsage (internal/harnessres/sample_windows.go) is the PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS field: peak non-paged-pool bytes the sampled process ever charged. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **byPool accumulator** - Local map[string]AccountRow / map[string]Account accumulator named byPool, paired with an order slice, built inside uniquePoolRows (internal/dispatchtick/accounts.go) and uniquePoolAccounts (internal/fleetaccounts/resolve.go): it keeps exactly one representative row per pool key, the best-ranked candidate under accountPoolRowLess / accountPoolLess, while the input rows are reduced down to one row per pool. |
| o | defined | doc-term | pool | **credential_pool.py (Hermes reference)** - The external Hermes script agent/credential_pool.py, named in the package comments of internal/metrics/spend.go and internal/spendrollup/spendrollup.go as the prior-art system that pools spend/credentials across accounts but relays the provider's own billed number without labeling who authored the figure. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **leaseWorkersByPool** - Function with two independent implementations (internal/dispatchtick/accounts.go over AccountRow/SeatLease, internal/fleetaccounts/resolve.go over Account/Lease) that walks the live seat leases and returns a map from pool key to the list of worker ids currently holding a lease in that pool; leaseLoadByPool and wave/status/resolve callers use it to seed per-pool in-flight occupancy before granting new slots. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **normalizeWorkerPool** - internal/dispatchtick/accounts.go helper (also called from tierroute.go) that normalizes raw account rows, drops non-routable ones, applies an optional product filter, and resolves the target model tier for a work kind - the shared front matter both RouteAccount and AllocateWave run before touching the pool. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **RotationEmptyPool** - accounts.RotationEmptyPool is the RotationNoCandidateReason constant ("empty_pool") that NextRotationDecision (internal/accounts/rotation.go) returns when a rotate finds NO eligible seats at all in the pool - every seat reserved, disabled, tombstoned, or unservable - and that cmd/fak/accounts_rotation_runtime.go switches on to render the failure to an operator. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **uniquePoolAccounts** - internal/fleetaccounts/resolve.go function that reduces a slice of Account rows to one representative Account per pool key via the byPool accumulator, keeping the best-ranked (offerable-first, then rank) row; called from AllocateWave, status_report.go, resolve.go, and capacity.go to avoid double-counting a pool's seats. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **usedPools** - Local map[string]bool set built during AllocateWave (internal/dispatchtick/accounts.go and internal/fleetaccounts/wave.go) that records which pool keys have already been granted at least one session slot in the current wave, so the result can report DistinctPools = len(usedPools) alongside the granted/shortfall counts and the reason string. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **Pool (polymodel residency)** - polymodel.NewPool (internal/polymodel/polymodel.go) constructs a model-RESIDENCY pool that keeps many prefill-warm models resident under one weight-byte budget, evicting the coldest UNPINNED model (LRU) when an admission would exceed the cap. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **DefaultPoolProfiles (cachemeta)** - cachemeta.DefaultPoolProfiles returns a representative PoolProfile per residency tier — HBM/DRAM/NUMA-far host-private, CXL the coherent switch-pooled zero-copy tier, disk local, remote reachable-but-copy-only — the order-of-magnitude default an operator overrides with their fabric's measured topology. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **PoolReuseVerdict (cachemeta gate)** - cachemeta.PoolReuseVerdict is the cross-tenant reuse trust GATE for a SHARED pool: it permits a tenant to attend a pooled cell another tenant wrote only when the cell is un-poisoned, declared shareable beyond its agent scope, adjudicated TaintTrusted, and its materialization key matches on every axis. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **PooledPrefillTokens (fleet-reuse compute)** - FleetReuseResult.PooledPrefillTokens (internal/cachemeta) is the prefill-token COMPUTE cost the chosen pool regime pays for one hot prefix shared by N tenants — one prefill under a reachable or fabric-shareable pool, N prefills when the tier is host-private. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **PooledResidentBytes (fleet-reuse capacity)** - FleetReuseResult.PooledResidentBytes (internal/cachemeta) is the resident-byte CAPACITY cost the chosen pool regime holds for one hot prefix shared by N tenants — ONE copy under a coherent fabric-shareable pool, N copies under a copy-only or host-private tier. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **WarmPoolFree (prewarm pollution gate)** - compute.PrewarmCandidate.WarmPoolFree is the pollution gate of the tool-latency prewarm admission decision: a bool that is true only when the lowest-eviction-priority warm KV pool has free capacity, so a WarmNow directive never displaces a demand session's resident prefix. |
| o | defined | symbol | pool | **SharedPool (fleet-benchmark catalog)** - FleetProfile.SharedPool (internal/turnbench) is a fleet-BENCHMARK knob: the number of DISTINCT shared reference reads the modeled agent fleet draws from (the shared catalog size), the parameter that makes the cross-agent dedup curve SATURATE as agent count rises. |
| o | defined | subsystem | pool | **cxlpooldemo (CXL pool demo)** - cmd/cxlpooldemo is a no-model, no-GPU command that proves the value of a SWITCH-POOLED multi-host shared memory tier (CXL.mem) by computing — purely over cachemeta tier/pool profiles — the three-way fleet economics (per-host private vs copy-only pool vs coherent CXL pool) and the cross-tenant trust gate. |

| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **RenderSystemdSystemUnit (Linux systemd system unit)** - systemservice RenderSystemdSystemUnit renders a PID-1-owned systemd system unit from a SystemdConfig, using DynamicUser=yes so systemd allocates a transient unprivileged identity while PID 1 retains lifecycle and durable-directory ownership; no login account or user manager is in the dependency chain, and hardening directives (NoNewPrivileges, ProtectSystem, ProtectKernelModules) lock the unit down. |

| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **Materialize (ctxplan view)** - ctxplan.Materialize re-derives a budgeted View of the lossless context store each turn: PlanCells picks which spans fit the budget, the selected ones are paged in through the trust gate, and a faithfulness Witness reconciles rendered-vs-refused so every elided span stays recoverable. |
| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **MaterializeLayout (ctxplan)** - ctxplan.MaterializeLayout is the layout-aware peer of Materialize: it uses a four-area Layout to choose the bounded candidate set and per-area precision, then renders the selected exact spans through the same trust gate and witness reconciliation as the default path. |
| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **MaterializeVerdict (cachemeta gate)** - cachemeta.MaterializeVerdict is the central cache gate: given the runtime materialization view, the stored Entry, the requested key, and quality evidence, it returns a LookupVerdict deciding whether a cached prefix/KV span may be served (Hit) or must be refused (Miss). |
| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **Materializer (memview)** - memview.Materializer is the producer type whose MaterializeSnippet turns a (source page, offset, length) selector into a provenance-bound MemoryViewRecord plus an admission Verdict, filling the record's Body only when the source page is trusted (Allow) and leaving it nil on a tainted source (Quarantine). |
| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **materialize*Tensors (model arch)** - materializeFalconTensors and its per-architecture siblings rewrite a loaded source/GGUF tensor manifest into fak's canonical tensor names (aliasing and splitting source-format tensors) for one model family before inference. |
| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **materializeTensorAliases (model)** - materializeTensorAliases applies the config's explicit canonical->source tensor-name aliases, creating zero-copy canonical manifest rows before any fused-tensor split, so a loader can point a canonical fused tensor at a source-format fused name. |
| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **RenderTurn (ctxplan seam)** - RenderTurn (on the agent CtxViewPlanner / SessionPlanner) re-renders a turn's message history through the ctxplan seam — materializing a budgeted View and paging resident spans back as the agent message list — and returns its input unchanged when the seam is disabled. |
| o | defined | symbol | render-materialize | **RenderedTokens (ctxplan View)** - ctxplan View.RenderedTokens sums the token cost of every span actually paged into the fresh history — the realized resident size, which is <= budget by construction. |

| o | defined | metric | score-debt | **negation_tax_debt (hot-path negation headline)** - internal/negframe/taxscore.go NegationTaxDebtKey: the FLAT headline count of reframable negation still broadcast on the hot path (the mechanical residual that survives each emit site's own reframe). |

| o | defined | metric | score-debt | **sota_debt (SOTA-coverage matrix debt)** - internal/sotacoverage/sotacoverage.go SOTADebt: the count of unmet cells in the state-of-the-art coverage matrix - capabilities the repo claims to match SOTA on but has no passing witness for. |
| o | defined | metric | score-debt | **negframe_debt** - The debt integer emitted by the negframe scorecard (internal/negframe DebtKey "negframe_debt"): the count of mechanical negative-frame steer-prose defects with a positive reframe attached, folded from the document corpus. |
| o | defined | metric | score-debt | **qa_process_debt** - The debt integer emitted by the qa-process scorecard (internal/qaprocessscore DebtKey "qa_process_debt"): HARD gaps in the test process - reverted landings with no regression test, packages below coverage floor - that are in-tree-mendable holes, not failing tests. |
| o | defined | symbol | score-debt | **skip_debt** - The boundarylint.SkipDebt rule (Code "SKIP_DEBT") that flags a test removing itself from the suite with a bare, unconditional t.Skip - a SOFT signal feeding the qa-process scorecard's skip_debt KPI, never a gate. |
| o | defined | metric | score-debt | **debt_by_kpi** - The standard scorecard payload field (json "debt_by_kpi"): a map[string]int breaking one scorecard's total debt down by its KPI names (lint, tests, structure, ...), emitted by every scorecard's corpus fold. |
| o | defined | metric | score-debt | **debt_delta** - The trend int field (DebtDelta / json "debt_delta") on cadencereport and milestoneburndown Trend structs: the signed change in a scorecard's debt between two cadence snapshots, and the docfreshrsi score component measuring rewrite paydown. |

| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **OnSlotFreed (scheduler callback)** - session.Scheduler.OnSlotFreed registers the single host callback the scheduler invokes once per slot-freed SlotEvent when a session transitions to a slot-releasing run-state. |

| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **PublishSessionRevision (change feed)** - Server.PublishSessionRevision records one drive-state revision onto the gateway's session change feed (#630) so /v1/fak/session/changes subscribers can drain DRIVE-state updates. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **serveSessions (drive table)** - serveSessions is the process-local, TraceID-keyed, bounded-LRU session.Table holding every served session's DRIVE state (run-state/budget/priority/pace), shared by the gateway session routes and any in-process agent loop. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **sessionAdmits (proxy admission gate)** - Server.sessionAdmits is the gateway's fallback proxy admission gate that reports whether a proxied request may proceed under the operator-controlled DRIVE run-state, failing OPEN when no ObserveSession hook is wired. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SlotCause (scheduler reason)** - session.SlotCause is the closed enum naming WHY a scheduling slot freed (budget-exhausted | paused | draining | stopped), carried on a SlotEvent and rendered as a lowercase token. |

| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **WithSessionTable (run option)** - agent.WithSessionTable is the RunOption that threads a per-session DRIVE-state session.Table plus the run's trace id into the agent loop so each turn honors the same budget/pace/run-state as a served request. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **handleFakSession (HTTP route)** - Server.handleFakSession is the gateway HTTP handler for /v1/fak/session/{id} — the read-write DRIVE-state control route that fans GET to observeSession and POST verbs to controlSession/steerSession. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **AccountSessionCap (capacity resolver)** - AccountSessionCap computes one product/account row's session capacity (max concurrent sessions) from product policy plus env overrides, called by both fleetaccounts and dispatchtick. |
| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **SessionCap (capacity field)** - SessionCap is the int field (json session_cap) on an account/fleet status row carrying its resolved session capacity, filled by AccountSessionCap. |
| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **SessionSlot (worker index)** - SessionSlot is the per-worker session-slot index field on dispatch-wave/account rows identifying which concurrent capacity slot a worker occupies. |
| o | defined | metric | session-runtime | **FreeSlots** - FreeSlots is the int field on fleet capacity/status rows counting currently-unleased session slots for an account or the fleet total. |
| o | defined | metric | session-runtime | **LeasedSlots** - LeasedSlots is the int field on fleet capacity/status rows counting currently-occupied session slots for an account or the fleet total. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **applySessionControl (table mutator)** - applySessionControl applies one parsed SessionControlRequest verb (pause/resume/priority/pace) to a session.Table entry, returning the resulting session.State. |
| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **AssumeSessionTurns** - AssumeSessionTurns is the `--assume-session-turns` knob feeding headSessionPrior's presumed session length when no bounded Budget.TurnsLeft horizon is wired. |
| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **DefaultAssumedSessionTurns** - DefaultAssumedSessionTurns is the const (=50) default session-length assumption the head-anchored compaction burst gate uses when AssumeSessionTurns is unset. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **headSessionPrior** - gateway.Server.headSessionPrior resolves the {TotalTurns, CurrentTurn} horizon pair the head-anchored burst gate reads when no bounded budget horizon is wired. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **BySession (fault counts)** - BySession is the toolprocgate console-fault report field breaking fault counts down per session (json by_session). |
| o | defined | metric | session-runtime | **BySessionType** - BySessionType is the cache-value report field attributing session/shape counts back to their session_type front door (guard|serve|run), present on both the Bucket aggregate and ShapeCluster. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **debugSessionVars** - debugSessionVars is the gateway package's type alias (= guardvars.SessionVars) for the per-session drive-state row that /debug/vars mirrors for each live session. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionVars (guardvars)** - guardvars.SessionVars describes one session's live drive-state; the gateway package reuses it verbatim as its debugSessionVars alias. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **defaultSessionRegistryPath** - defaultSessionRegistryPath resolves the default on-disk session-registry path when neither $FAK_SESSION_REGISTRY nor a --registry flag is given. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **DevSessionBenefit (report field)** - DevSessionBenefit is the cache-value Track-3 report field (*DevSessionBenefitReport) holding the dev-session cache-benefit lens, deliberately never summed into FleetBenefit or Track 2. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **DevSessionBenefitReport** - DevSessionBenefitReport is the struct type folded by FoldDevSessionBenefit and rendered by RenderDevSessionBenefit, carrying Track-3 dev-session cache-benefit numbers. |
