| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **SessionFile (image filename)** - SessionFile is the sessionimage package const naming the fixed session.json file inside a session image's CAS layout. |
| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **maxSessionRespBytes** - maxSessionRespBytes bounds the byte size of one session's rendered HTTP/CLI response (accounts-rehome, session ls, signal). |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **NewScheduler (constructor)** - NewScheduler is the constructor name shared, independently, by internal/session (NewScheduler(policy) building a *Scheduler over StrictPriority/WeightedFair) and internal/microagent (NewScheduler(k) building a fixed-K local slot scheduler). |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **parseHookSessionID** - parseHookSessionID extracts a session id from a Claude Code hook JSON payload, used by the precompact/stophook guard actuators. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **persistServeSessionRevision** - persistServeSessionRevision persists a served session's current session.State to the durable session store as a new revision, called from the CLI session-control path. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **runSessionEnvelope** - runSessionEnvelope is the actuator behind `fak session envelope`, producing a resume/handoff envelope for a session. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **selfSession** - selfSession is the leaseref parameter naming the CURRENT process's own session id, threaded through ClassifyLiveness/ClassifyLive so a lease can be told apart from a peer's. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionAccount** - gateway.SessionAccount describes one Claude subscription seat in the on-box roster shown by SessionEndpoints, marked with how it is currently used. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **sessionActivity** - sessionActivity (built by newSessionActivity) is the gateway package's private tracker recording each live trace's in-flight/idle age, projected onto debugSessionVars. |
| o | defined | subsystem | session-runtime | **sessionctl (package)** - internal/sessionctl owns the redirect control op (out-of-band objective-change, #2755/#2756): payload, validation, and the per-session next-boundary mailbox plus live-objective store. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionEndpoints** - gateway.SessionEndpoints aggregates the roster `fak info`/guard-endpoints surfaces render: Accounts, Nodes, and an embedded Harness together. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionFleetMachine** - gateway.SessionFleetMachine is one machine's row within the GATED-WIP SessionFleet aggregate, folded from a fleetpane.FleetDoc snapshot. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionHarness** - gateway.SessionHarness is embedded in SessionEndpoints, describing the harness/runner surface (set via a SetSessionHarnessProvider hook) shown alongside accounts and nodes. |
| o | defined | concept | session-runtime | **SessionLedger (planned, #2392)** - A not-yet-built append-only session-scoped ledger referenced only as a forward pointer (#2392) from atif/doc.go, session/scratch_lease.go, and worklog/worklog.go; those call sites deliberately stay silent on entry format pending that work. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionNode** - gateway.SessionNode describes one machine/node entry within SessionEndpoints' roster. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionRow** - sidecar.SessionRow is the census row type: one agent session joined across the four key data sources for `fak sidecar`. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **sessionsCodexLoop** - sessionsCodexLoop is the actuator behind `fak sessions codex-loop`, diagnosing repeated-goal-failure loops from a Codex TUI JSONL session's history. |
| o | defined | subsystem | session-runtime | **sessionsearch (package)** - internal/sessionsearch performs witnessed cross-session recall (a pure TF-IDF fold) over the guard tool-process journal, with cache-safe suffix-only injection and a usefulness witness (#2913). |
| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **SessionStart (Claude Code hook)** - SessionStart is the Claude Code hook event name fak installs/actuates (guard_sessionstart.go) to inject a one-line MCP-verb discoverability hint into the first turn. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SetSessionEndpointsProvider** - gateway.Server.SetSessionEndpointsProvider wires a SessionEndpoints producer function into the server so /debug/vars and the endpoints route can render it. |
| o | defined | concept | session-runtime | **Supersession (note retirement)** - The structural retirement relation behind a memory note's author-declared supersedes edge, resolved by ResolveSupersession so a withheld (superseded) note stops surfacing while its own supersedes edge still retires its target. |
| o | defined | config | session-runtime | **tuiSessionsSchema** - tuiSessionsSchema is the const naming the TUI overview-sessions payload schema version (fak.tui.sessions.v1). |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **ScratchJournal** - session.ScratchJournal is the append-only ledger interface a scratchpad lifecycle records onto TODAY - the real, currently-implemented mechanism SessionLedger (#2392) is a planned future generalization of. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **sessionFeed** - sessionFeed is the gateway's bounded ring of SessionChangeEvents fed by the host's PublishSessionRevision, drained by the /v1/fak/session/changes subscribe route. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **tuiSessionReport** - tuiSessionReport is the TUI overview-sessions report struct built by buildTUISessionReport from a gateway SessionListResponse, carrying the rows, counts, and lanes the TUI sessions view renders. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **ParentSessionID** - ParentSessionID is the json field (parent_session_id) on eveimport and Codex-loop session records identifying the parent session that spawned a subagent, enabling parent-child session linkage. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **sessionStartSHA** - sessionStartSHA captures the git HEAD SHA at session start, used as the progress-head baseline by the guard no-progress detector and the durable-session setup. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **StoppedBySession** - StoppedBySession is the ArmMetrics field (json stopped_by_session) carrying the session-control stop reason (BudgetTurns, BudgetContext, Paused, Drained, Terminated) when a wired session.Table ended the arm. |
| o | defined | subsystem | session-runtime | **sessionreplay (package)** - internal/sessionreplay freezes one turn's regime-conditioned harness decision as a fak.sessionreplay.v1 fixture and re-adjudicates it through the REAL adjudicator over an internal/policy regime floor for regression testing. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **handleFakSession** - handleFakSession is the HTTP handler behind POST/GET /v1/fak/session/{id}/{verb}, the session DRIVE-state control surface routing pause/resume/priority/pace/subscribe/observe verbs. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **launchHostSessionPlatform** - launchHostSessionPlatform is the S4U watchdog's session-0 resurrection launcher, called by resurrectHostCrashSessions to hand a typed hostresurrect.Request to the interactive broker after a host crash. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **Budget** - A session's remaining work allotment across three independent axes: turns (round-trips), output tokens, and context tokens. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **Pace** - The per-turn throttle: max output tokens and minimum gap between turns, applied cooperatively without pausing the session. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **RunState** - A served session's lifecycle position in a small total state machine: Running, Throttled, Paused, Draining, or Stopped. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionID** - The stable identity key for a session (its TraceID), constant across all of the session's turns. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **NewSession** - The constructor that admits a fresh session into the table with its initial drive state. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionTurn** - One accounted served round-trip bound to a session: the begin / debit / served lifecycle of a single turn. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionUsage** - Per-session token and cost accounting rolled up across the session's turns. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionControlRequest** - An external steer directive against a live session: pause / resume / priority / pace change. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **SessionReset** - A directive to clear a session's drive state back to a clean baseline without removing it from the pool. |
| o | defined | symbol | session-runtime | **ListSessions** - The read query that returns a snapshot of all live sessions in the table. |
| o | defined | concept | support-maturity | **SupportMaturity Rung (M0-M7 ladder)** - supportmaturity.Rung is the uint8-backed closed M0-M7 support-maturity ladder type (M0 unsupported through M7 beyond-SOTA, with M6Parity the SOTA-local parity rung), and FromSupport lowers a covmatrix Support value onto that ladder (UNDEFINED/FENCED/PROOF-PATH/SUPPORTED to M0/M1/M3/M4). |
| o | defined | metric | support-maturity | **MaturityScore (cadence maturity fold)** - cadencereport MaturityScore is the LedgerRow integer rating of the feature-lifecycle scorecard, produced by CollectMaturity (which builds and converts the Go-native maturity scorecard in-process) and merged into the cadence envelope by FoldWithMaturity. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **ExactSpanSupported (engine evict capability)** - ExactSpanSupported (enginecache Result field, set by SupportsExactSpan) reports whether a documented serving engine or KV pool can evict exactly the named K/V span instead of resetting the whole prefix cache, the same capability bit SupportsRaw exposes for a paged KV pool that carries the Kraw plane. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **interpretmaturity (support-maturity identifier)** - interpretmaturity is the concrete support-maturity identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring support-maturity concepts. |
| o | defined | subsystem | support-maturity | **arch_support.go (in-kernel architecture support surface)** - internal/model/arch_support.go: the surface stating which checkpoint architectures fak's in-kernel forward actually implements - it carries the forward-path classification and the typed refusal for anything it does not. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **UnsupportedArchError (typed unsupported-arch refusal)** - internal/model/arch_support.go: the typed refusal newModel returns when a checkpoint carries weights for an architecture whose in-kernel forward fak does not yet implement, so `fak serve` can surface a named refusal before serving a token instead of binding the gateway and panicking on the first turn. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **CoverageReport (four same-named per-package coverage folds)** - CoverageReport is a REUSED name, not one type: internal/accountprobe grades an enrolled seat roster (and separately reports whether the ledger said anything at all); internal/issuefanout is the two-rate adoption scorecard with per-leaf evidence; internal/productscorecard.CoverageReport is a FUNCTION over a catalog; and cmd/fak/route_coverage.go's lowercase coverageReport renders model-route coverage. |
| o | defined | config | support-maturity | **DefaultCoverageScanCap (fan-out issue scan bound)** - internal/issuefanout/coverage.go: the default cap on issues scanned when measuring fan-out coverage (20000). It is deliberately NOT the dedupe bound - fan-out markers cluster in OLDER issues, so a small window returns zero markers and reports a confident 0% that is simply wrong; a scan that hits the cap is marked truncated and forces OK false. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **LeafCoverage (per-leaf fan-out evidence row)** - internal/issuefanout/coverage.go: ONE leaf's evidence row inside the fan-out coverage scorecard - the per-leaf witness behind the headline rates. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **CoveragePct** - CoveragePct (internal/commitsubject/coverage.go) is the coverage-percentage field of the commit-subject coverage payload. |
| o | defined | config | support-maturity | **minCoverage** - minCoverage (internal/commitsubject, internal/toolcoverage) is the optional minimum-coverage floor the coverage payload builders grade against. |
| o | defined | subsystem | support-maturity | **qwen36parity** - Package qwen36parity (internal/qwen36parity) loads and grades qwen3.6 parity witness reports against a required ratio. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **RouterCoverage** - RouterCoverage (internal/dispatchtick/router.go) reports how much of the issue backlog the dispatch router actually fetched against its limit. |
| o | defined | subsystem | support-maturity | **sotacoverage** - Package sotacoverage (internal/sotacoverage) drives the sota-coverage scorecard (sota_debt) over the prior-art matrix. |
| o | defined | subsystem | support-maturity | **toolcoverage** - Package toolcoverage (internal/toolcoverage) audits which load-bearing tools modules have sibling tests. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **CorrectnessLaw (vCache cache-safety statement)** - CorrectnessLaw is the vCache action/report field that states the correctness invariant: full uncached prompt execution remains the authoritative path and cache hits are only rebates. |
| o | defined | subsystem | support-maturity | **supportmaturity (support-maturity rung scale)** - internal/supportmaturity (internal/milestonereport uses M4Correct): the support-maturity rung scale (M0..M4) grading how mature a subsystem's support is; maturedRung = M4Correct is the top rung a milestone requires. |
| o | defined | subsystem | support-maturity | **dsparity** - Package dsparity (internal/dsparity) is the pure, offline, dependency-free parity-harness SPECIFICATION for future DeepSeek-V4 native kernels (#3021): it encodes the parity rows and expected fields (invariance axis, tolerance, witness kind) a batch-invariant kernel must satisfy before a speed claim can close, runnable without real weights or a GPU. |
| o | defined | subsystem | support-maturity | **eveparity** - Package eveparity (internal/eveparity) is the CI-runnable witness for issue #2605: it runs a fixture Eve-shaped eval suite once raw and once fak-routed through the real gateway proxy, then Compare folds the two arms into a Witness proving fak never silently downgrades a hard Eve gate failure into a soft pass. |
| o | defined | config | support-maturity | **MONITOR_NO_FAILURE_COVERAGE** - The closed arm-time refusal token (ReasonMonitorNoFailureCoverage, abi.ReasonCode 1044, internal/toolproc/monitor.go) ArmMonitor cites when a monitor's filter declares no failure-signature class alongside its progress pattern, so a monitor that would stay silent-on-failure by construction is refused instead of armed. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **ParityVerdict (eveparity witness)** - The eveparity.Witness.ParityVerdict string field ('pass' | 'divergent') Compare sets: pass only when every case agrees on pass/fail with a byte-identical reason and no hard-gate failure was downgraded to a soft score; any disagreement or downgrade flips it to divergent. |
| o | defined | symbol | support-maturity | **ParityVerdict (recall replica agreement)** - The recall package's uint8 enum (ParityAgree | ParityResolved | ParityConflict) ParityFinding.Verdict carries: whether several replicas of one logical memory resource corroborate on bytes, resolve to one clear live witness, or conflict with no clear winner (#786). |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **loopmgr witnessed run-status set** - The loop/bench witnessed run-status family: loopmgr.StatusWitnessRefused ("witness_refused") and StatusWitnessUnavailable ("witness_unavailable"), bench/loopgate VerdictWitnessed/OutcomeWitnessed ("witnessed") and its witnessed_done promotion, callavoid's WitnessedRedirect and its WitnessedEmpty count, and loopindex's WitnessedIndex health score. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **unwitnessed-claim reason set** - The unwitnessed-claim deny/re-arm reason family: loopgate.ReasonDoneUnwitnessed ("LOOP_DONE_UNWITNESSED", the re-arm reason when a done claim has no witness, as adjudicated for a claim-unwitnessed turn) and the proposed context-safety SAFETY_UNWITNESSED DOS refusal token for a rendered number with no tamper-evident source. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ReviewRefute (scout review verdict)** - modelroute.ReviewRefute is the scout-review verdict value "refute" returned when an opt-in pre-commit review rejects a diff; safecommit raises it to the ReasonReviewRefuted ("REVIEW_REFUTED") refusal that blocks the commit before it lands. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessFor (support-maturity prover)** - supportmaturity.WitnessFor returns the exact non-author WitnessKind that must PROVE a given support-maturity rung, the binding the promotion check enforces (the evidence kind must be precisely the witness WitnessFor names). |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **EventWitness (loop ledger event kind)** - loopmgr.EventWitness is the loop-ledger event KIND carrying a run's external verdict (witnessed_done / witness_refused / witness_unavailable), the row that supplies the independent done-evidence the loopverify corpus folds beside each self-reported EventEnd. |
| o | defined | subsystem | witness-proof | **cachewitness (KV-prefix value seam)** - internal/cachewitness is the observation seam that folds the WITNESSED kv_prefix metric family (KVPrefixWitness.ReuseRatio over fak_gateway_kv_prefix_reused_tokens_total) into a provenance-labeled Record, keeping WITNESSED reuse strictly distinct from OBSERVED provider cache_read and exposing the honesty-fence SingleSessionMarginalX. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessResult (loopgate evidence summary)** - loopgate.WitnessResult is the witness adapter's evidence summary struct (Outcome, Reason, Detail, RawVerdict, Rung) that Adjudicate consumes to accept a done claim only on OutcomeWitnessed, parsed from the dos commit-audit/verify JSON. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **witnessPrefix (blob-hash label)** - syspromptmmu.witnessPrefix is the constant "blob-sha256:" that labels a syspromptmmu Witness string as a content-blob hash (not a git object id), so a system-prompt span's admitting witness is recognizable as a content digest. |
| o | defined | doc-term | witness-proof | **TestPrefixReuseFanoutWitness (fanbench soundness proof)** - TestPrefixReuseFanoutWitness is the cmd/fanbench soundness test that grounds the fan-out prefix-reuse GEOMETRY claim: it prefills the master-goal prefix once and proves N cloned sub-agents are bit-identical to an independent full prefill (max|delta|=0). |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **TallyFromCountersWitnessed (realized-deny fold)** - callavoid.TallyFromCountersWitnessed is TallyFromCounters with a set of WITNESSED productive denies (each a WitnessedRedirect carrying its enumerated variant set), netting witnessed denies so each deny is counted exactly once and only a non-forgeable, deduplicated fan-out is credited into the graded headline. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **contextwitnessed (witness-proof identifier)** - contextwitnessed is the concrete witness-proof identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring witness-proof concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **kernelwitnessed (witness-proof identifier)** - kernelwitnessed is the concrete witness-proof identifier discovered in the tree and positioned here so the scorecard distinguishes it from neighboring witness-proof concepts. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ProgressWitnessedAt (resume watchdog recovery timestamp)** - ProgressWitnessedAt is the resume watchdog MTTR timestamp proving a resumed session made independent progress after launch, derived from ledger progress, new turns, or a commit witness. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **DISCARD_WITNESSED (wip-recon safe-discard action)** - internal/wiprecon/recon.go: the reconciliation ACTION for a crashed owner's checkpoint whose delta already LANDED in HEAD (git-witnessed), so dropping it loses nothing. Fail-safe by construction: the only path to it requires Landed==true. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **validWitness (two same-named witness membership tests)** - validWitness is a REUSED name for two different membership tests: internal/dsparity's map of accepted parity Witness values, and internal/trajctl's function over a WitnessRung. Both answer 'is this witness value in the closed set', over unrelated vocabularies. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **witnessExitedWorkers (post-run commit-claim audit)** - cmd/fak/dispatch_tick_witness.go: audits the workers that have EXITED, bucketing each resolve log into witnessed / unwitnessed / no-commit claims and returning the per-worker witness records. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ClaimWitnessed (dispatch-tick sweep verdict)** - dispatchtick.ClaimWitnessed is the CLAIM_WITNESSED verdict a finished dispatch worker's slot grades into when `dos commit-audit` confirms its resolving commit cleared the diff-witnessed keep-bit - the positive branch of the .witness sidecar claim vocabulary (ClaimWitnessed / ClaimUnwitnessed / ClaimNoCommit). |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessClaim** - WitnessClaim (internal/taskmgr/evidence.go) is the witness interface method: turn a Claim into a WitnessRecord backed by evidence. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **PathWitness** - PathWitness (internal/taskmgr/evidence.go) witnesses a claim by checking that the claimed filesystem path actually exists. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ShapeWitness** - ShapeWitness (internal/taskmgr/shapewitness.go) witnesses a claim by checking the artifact's declared shape (for example SLO fields), not just its presence. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessTask** - Manager.WitnessTask (internal/taskmgr/evidence.go) runs a witness over a task's claim and stores the resulting WitnessRecord with evidence refs. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **cloneWitness** - cloneWitness (internal/taskmgr/evidence.go) is the deep-copy helper for a WitnessRecord, so handed-off evidence cannot alias the stored record. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **CoreLockWitness** - CoreLockWitness (internal/safecommit/safecommit.go) is the result field carrying the witness claim under which a core-lock hold was cleared. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **CoreLockMaintenanceWitness** - CoreLockMaintenanceWitness (internal/safecommit) is the commit option: an independent witness claim that may clear a core-lock maintenance hold. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **HasWitness** - HasWitness (internal/closurerate/closurerate.go) is the bit recording whether an issue close points at a diff, commit, or test witness. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **MissingWitness** - MissingWitness (internal/devexmeter) lists the friction classes whose meter window lacked a witness, keeping the meter honest about gaps. |
| o | defined | config | witness-proof | **LOOP_DONE_UNWITNESSED** - LOOP_DONE_UNWITNESSED (internal/gateway/adjudicate_proposed.go ReasonLoopBodyUnwitnessed) is the closed refusal token for a loop body claiming done with no witness. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **dos_witness_verbs** - dos_witness_verbs.go (internal/hooks) is fak's bounded mirror of the DOS commit-audit claim classifier's code-effect verb set. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **EvidenceTrustEpoch** - EvidenceTrustEpoch (internal/recall) is the recall trust axis: the integrity epoch a page was admitted under must still be current. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **EvidenceWitness** - EvidenceWitness (internal/recall) is the recall trust axis: the external trust witness a page was admitted under must still verify. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **origin_witness** - origin_witness (internal/taskmgr, /fak/tasks filter) is the evidence axis naming WHICH witness vouched for a task's origin claim. |
| o | defined | config | witness-proof | **ReasonReviewRefuted** - ReasonReviewRefuted (internal/safecommit) is the refusal token REVIEW_REFUTED: an opt-in scout review refuted the diff before commit. |
| o | defined | subsystem | witness-proof | **unwitnessedclaim** - Package unwitnessedclaim (internal/unwitnessedclaim) buckets issue-close claims that carry no witness, for `fak unwitnessed-claim`. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **witnessed_numbers** - witnessed_numbers (`fak dispatch progress --json`) is the JSON key listing issue numbers whose close was witnessed by commit links. |
| o | defined | config | witness-proof | **witnessJSON (--witness-json)** - witnessJSON (cmd/fak/dispatch_commit_links.go) is the --witness-json flag: a path of commit-linked issue witness facts to bucket close-gate failures from. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessWindow** - WitnessWindow (internal/cachewitness/cachewitness.go) is the bounded record window a cache-usage witness folds over. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessCommands (observed proof commands)** - WitnessCommands is the recorded list of build, test, or verification commands observed in fleet/session summaries as evidence for a claimed change. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessOK (diff-witnessed constant)** - WitnessOK is the dispatchtick constant value diff-witnessed that marks a claimed resolution as corroborated by the required witness rung. |
| o | defined | subsystem | witness-proof | **buildwitness (default-build compile guard)** - internal/buildwitness is a structural CI-guard package whose test runs `go build ./cmd/fak` under default build tags from a clean checkout, proving the default build is green independent of any WIP file left tagless in the shared tree. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **CommitWitnessed (dispatch-tick audit-row grader)** - internal/dispatchtick.CommitWitnessed(verdict, witness string) bool grades one `dos commit-audit` row into a dispatch-tick worker slot's productive keep-bit, true only when verdict is OK AND witness equals the diff-witnessed constant WitnessOK. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **InvalidationExternalRefutation (cachemeta invalidation-mode)** - internal/cachemeta.InvalidationExternalRefutation ("external_refutation") is the InvalidationMode enum value recording that a cache entry's Coherence.InvalidationMode was set because its bound external world-state witness was refuted, as opposed to LRU, TTL, write-epoch, or policy eviction. |
| o | defined | metric | witness-proof | **MissingWitnesses (roadmap lane debt count)** - internal/milestonereport.MissingWitnesses (int, mirrored into internal/operatorbrief) is the per-lane roadmap COUNT of epics/issues lacking a witness, weighted 3x into the lane's DebtScore. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **NoKeyWitnessCommand (vcache no-key CLI command)** - cmd/fak's vcacheContextAPIStatus.NoKeyWitnessCommand field documents the exact CLI command ("fak vcache context-witness") a caller without a live provider API key can run to witness vCache context savings. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **OpenWitnessed (closure-audit bucket)** - internal/closureaudit.OpenWitnessed ("OPEN_WITNESSED") is the closure-audit BUCKET assigned to an issue that is still open on GitHub but already carries a Resolving-kind witnessed commit - the bucket the close arm drives to CLOSED. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ProvenanceWitnessed (context-envelope evidence tier)** - internal/ctxplan.ProvenanceWitnessed ("WITNESSED") is the strongest of four ordered evidence-provenance labels (FALLBACK < MODELED < OBSERVED < WITNESSED) on an EffectiveContextEnvelope row, meaning the row's default budget came from a local same-task measured run rather than a prior or external telemetry. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ReasonLoopBodyUnwitnessed (gateway constant name)** - internal/gateway.ReasonLoopBodyUnwitnessed is the Go constant declared in adjudicate_proposed.go (and reused by subagent_witness.go for the subagent fold boundary) whose string value is the wire reason LOOP_DONE_UNWITNESSED. |
| o | defined | metric | witness-proof | **recall_value_witnessed (memvaluescore KPI key)** - internal/memvaluescore scores a KPI row keyed "recall_value_witnessed" (Group "value") as a binary pass/fail on whether the recall-events ledger has any witnessed rows at all - rows `fak memory recall` appends only when a real recall witnesses an event. |
| o | defined | doc-term | witness-proof | **Refuting (causalbench TARGET causal-targeting check)** - cmd/causalbench's step-4 "TARGET" assertion is the causal-targeting property that calling Revoke on one witness must evict ONLY entries admitted under that witness - refuting an unrelated witness must evict zero unrelated entries, proving eviction is causally targeted rather than a blunt flush. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **VerifyWitness (model-route verification tier)** - internal/modelroute.VerifyWitness (Verification = "witness") is the strongest model-route verification tier, meaning DOS confirmed the work from git evidence (dos verify / dos commit-audit); Trusted() and Witnessed() both gate on it above VerifyJudge. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **warmWitness (cacheprice shed-token parameter)** - internal/cacheprice.ShedTokenEquiv's warmWitness parameter is the OBSERVED provider cache_read count witnessing how many shed compaction tokens the provider was already serving warm; the function blends min(shed, warmWitness) at ReadMultiplier and the remainder at full input price. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessDosVerify (playbook evidence-kind)** - internal/contextq.WitnessDosVerify (WitnessKind = "dos_verify") is the playbook WitnessKind enum value meaning a loop-ledger counter increment was earned because the referenced work was independently confirmed via a dos_verify / `fak <leaf>` ship-stamp, not merely claimed. |
| o | defined | metric | witness-proof | **WitnessedOpen (loop-economics still-open count)** - cmd/fak's loopEconWitnessed.WitnessedOpen (int64, JSON "witnessed_open") is the ledger-derived COUNT of issues in the OpenWitnessed closure-audit bucket at report time, rendered in `fak loop economics` as "witnessed-done still open". |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessKind (support-maturity evidence enum)** - internal/supportmaturity.WitnessKind (uint8) is the enum of evidence kinds - produced per-Rung by WitnessFor - that PROVES a support-maturity rung promotion is earned rather than merely claimed. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessSidecarSuffix (dispatch-tick audited-once marker)** - internal/dispatchtick.WitnessSidecarSuffix (".witness") is the on-disk file-name suffix marking a dispatch-tick worker slot as audited-once, since a landed commit's diff (and so its verdict) is immutable and a slot must be graded exactly one time. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessSource (concept-bench referee field)** - internal/conceptbench's Verdict.WitnessSource is a string field naming the exact real DOS tool that answered one concept-bench probe (e.g. dos_verify, dos_arbitrate, dos_check_reason), so a probe's verdict can be traced back to which referee produced it. |
| o | defined | subsystem | witness-proof | **worktreewitness (detached-trunk command harness)** - internal/worktreewitness runs an arbitrary command inside a transient, self-reaping detached git worktree pinned at the resolved origin/main SHA, so the verdict reflects the trunk tip and is never contaminated by a peer's dirty uncommitted WIP in the shared working tree. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **VerdictRequireWitness (abi verdict)** - abi.VerdictRequireWitness is the kernel verdict kind that leaves a gated call PENDING an independent read-back, so the kernel must corroborate the caller's claimed effect (carried in the WitnessPayload) before the call may dispatch. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ReasonUnwitnessed (deny reason)** - abi.ReasonUnwitnessed is the deny reason-code the kernel attaches when a require-witness gate found no corroboration, so the fail-closed default turns the pending verdict into a DENY (also surfaced as looprecover's unwitnessed disposition). |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessPayload (verdict claim carrier)** - abi.WitnessPayload is the typed bounded-disclosure verdict payload struct{Claim string} that an adjudicator attaches to a RequireWitness or Deny verdict to carry the single claim string the kernel must corroborate. |
| o | defined | concept | witness-proof | **refute (revocation trigger)** - refute is the action of calling vdso.Revoke(witness) on the integrity/coherence bus to mark a previously-trusted world-state witness as contradicted, stranding every cache entry admitted under it. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ProofRefuted (callavoid status)** - callavoid.ProofRefuted is the two-valued proof-status value 'REFUTED' meaning a cost-benefit proof concluded that avoidance/memoization does not pay (single-use, too-volatile, or validation as costly as the call). |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessRecord (taskmgr evidence)** - taskmgr.WitnessRecord is the evidence-backed verdict struct a Witness attaches BESIDE a task/step's claimed State (VerifiedState plus source and evidence refs), so a snapshot holds both 'process says done' and 'a witness has/has not confirmed it'. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessConfirmed (witness outcome)** - abi.WitnessConfirmed is the WitnessOutcome value meaning a resolver INDEPENDENTLY CORROBORATED the claimed effect from evidence the agent did not author, which opens the require-witness gate. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessRefuted (witness outcome)** - abi.WitnessRefuted is the WitnessOutcome value meaning a resolver found evidence that CONTRADICTS the claimed effect (the agent's claim was false), keeping the require-witness gate closed. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessAbstain (witness outcome)** - abi.WitnessAbstain is the zero-value WitnessOutcome meaning the resolver had NO evidence either way (fail-to-abstain), e.g. an unrecognized claim or git unavailable, which the kernel's fail-closed default then turns into a deny. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **defaultWitnessOf (vdso extractor)** - vdso.defaultWitnessOf is the default extractor that derives the external world-state witness string a call/result was admitted under (from the call/result meta), used by the coherence bus when no per-tool adapter is registered. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **resolveWitness (kernel gate driver)** - kernel.resolveWitness drives the require-witness gate: on a RequireWitness verdict it asks every registered abi.WitnessResolver to corroborate the claim and folds their WitnessOutcomes into a final allow/deny. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ShapeGLMTurnSegmentWitnessed (coherence shaper)** - cachemeta.ShapeGLMTurnSegmentWitnessed is the simplest live-path coherence shaper: given a turn's prompt segments and a revoked(witness) predicate, it emits the prefix-break directive that severs the provider prefix at the first refuted span. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **StatusWitnessedDone (loop run status)** - loopmgr.StatusWitnessedDone is the loop run-status 'witnessed_done' meaning an independent witness event confirmed the run's claimed completion (carrying evidence refs), promoting it above claimed_done. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **SubscribeRevocations (vdso observer seam)** - vdso.SubscribeRevocations registers an observer callback that fires on every refutation (Revocation) published to the integrity bus and returns a cancel func, never firing on the read hot path. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessEntry (session-image keep-bit)** - sessionimage.WitnessEntry binds one effect's keep-bit (a taskmgr.WitnessRecord) to a stable EffectID inside a persisted/restored session image, so a rehydrated session knows which effects were witnessed. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessFunc (vdso witness adapter)** - vdso.WitnessFunc is a per-tool adapter func(c *ToolCall, r *Result) string registered via RegisterWitness that extracts the external world-state witness a specific tool's result was admitted under, overriding the default extraction. |
| o | defined | metric | witness-proof | **WitnessGaps (TUI counter)** - WitnessGaps is the TUI loop-overview counter tallying runs that ended or claimed done but were never witnessed — the per-report aggregate of the unwitnessed gap, weighted into the attention score. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **StatusWitnessRefused (loop run status)** - loopmgr.StatusWitnessRefused is the loop run-status 'witness_refused' (and its WitnessRefused counter) meaning a witness RAN and the evidence contradicted the run's claimed completion. |
| o | defined | metric | witness-proof | **MissWitnessRevoked (vdso cache-miss)** - vdso.MissWitnessRevoked is the cache-miss reason 'WITNESS_REVOKED' counted when a cached entry was admitted under a now-refuted external witness and therefore fails closed instead of serving stale bytes. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **VerifiedUnavailable (witness unavailable)** - taskmgr.VerifiedUnavailable (and loopmgr.StatusWitnessUnavailable) is the state where a witness was ASKED but could not read the effect back — no network, a missing ref — so the claim is neither confirmed nor refused and must not downgrade to claimed-done. |
| o | defined | doc-term | witness-proof | **gpuServerGlmGpuWitness (CUDA GPU proof)** - gpuServerGlmGpuWitness is the on-GPU server CUDA GPU correctness witness for the GLM-MoE-DSA backend forward (TestCUDAGLMMoeDsaBackendForward run via private GPU witness runner), invoked because the win32 build host cannot execute the CUDA path. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **ProviderTransportWitness (provider-cache action evidence)** - vcacheobserve.ProviderTransportWitness is the evidence struct a caller supplies to `PlanProviderActionsWithOptions`, carrying provider-cache transport capability, byte-identical prefix, deletion capability, and source labels that can upgrade spendful vCache action rows from gated to ready. |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **WitnessOutcome** - The result of claim corroboration: Confirmed (evidence supports it), Refuted (evidence contradicts it), or Abstain (no definitive evidence). |
| o | defined | symbol | witness-proof | **TrustEpoch** - A monotonically-increasing integrity clock that increments with each witness refutation, dual to WorldVersion (the consistency clock). |

## Concept roll-up (the namespace at its abstraction heads)

Most readers cannot hold every concept at once. The optional `parent` forest lets the catalog roll concepts up to the abstraction that HEADS them - and the roll-up is **weakest-link**: an abstraction reads as crystal only when *every* concept beneath it is. A single `defined` leaf keeps the whole head from rolling up to crystal, so the collapsed view can never hide fog it contains. `!` marks a head whose declared verdict reads clearer than its subtree supports.
